# Thay đổi bài B2–C2 (2026-11-06)

So sánh từng bài với bản trước khi sửa. Mỗi bài liệt kê: bảng ngữ pháp, bài tập ngữ pháp, bài dịch ghép thẻ, chấm bài viết, và các phần khác nếu có đổi. Mũi tên → là trước → sau.
Tổng: 176 bài có thay đổi.


## B2

### b2-01-1
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Thì | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Quá khứ tiếp diễn | Bối cảnh đang diễn ra: the tide was going out | wasn't / weren't + V-ing
    - Quá khứ hoàn thành | Việc đã xong trước một mốc: had already attacked | hadn't + V3
    - QKHT tiếp diễn | Việc kéo dài trước một mốc: had been studying for months | hadn't been + V-ing
  - Sau:
    - Quá khứ tiếp diễn | The Mongol fleet was sailing back down the river. | The tide wasn't rising any more.
    - Quá khứ hoàn thành | The Mongol army had already attacked Dai Viet twice. | The enemy hadn't seen the stakes under the water.
    - QKHT tiếp diễn | He had been studying the tides for months. | The Mongols hadn't been expecting a trap.
- **BT4 (reorder)** [He had been studying the tides for months before the battle.] : nhận thêm ["Before the battle he had been studying the tides for months."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["had been","'d been"] → ["had been","hadn't been","had not been","i'd been","we'd been","they'd been","he'd been","she'd been"]

### b2-01-2
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Không nói
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Never / Rarely / Seldom | Never + trợ động từ + chủ ngữ + V: Never had I imagined … | Không viết: Never I had imagined …
    - Not until … | Not until + mệnh đề, did + chủ ngữ + V: Not until I stood there did I understand. | Đảo ngữ nằm ở mệnh đề thứ hai.
    - Only then / Only after … | Only then did I realise … | Thì đơn mượn do / does / did: Never did I expect …
  - Sau:
    - Never / Rarely / Seldom | Never had I imagined the cells would be so small. | Never I had imagined the cells would be so small.
    - Not until + mệnh đề | Not until I stood there did I understand. | Not until I stood there I understood.
    - Only then / Only after … | Only then did I realise how brave they were. | Only then I realised how brave they were.
    - Thì đơn → mượn did | Never did I expect the prison to feel so cold. | Never I expected the prison to feel so cold.
- **BT1 (fill)** : nhận thêm ["had"]; giải thích "Rarely + have + I + felt (hiện tại hoàn thành đảo ngữ)." → "Rarely + have / had + I + felt (đảo ngữ hiện tại hoàn thành hoặc quá khứ hoàn thành)."
- **BT3 (fix)** [Seldom have I heard such a brave story.] : đề "Câu sau có một lỗi. Hãy viết lại cho đúng." → "Câu sau có một lỗi đảo ngữ. Hãy viết lại cho đúng, vẫn bắt đầu bằng "Seldom"."
- **BT4 (reorder)** [Never had I imagined the cells were so small.] : đề "Sắp xếp các từ thành câu đúng." → "Sắp xếp thành câu đảo ngữ, bắt đầu bằng "Never"."
- **Dịch T2** [Never had I imagined the cells were so dark.]: nhận thêm ["I never imagined the cells were so dark."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Never have","Never had","Never did","Rarely","Seldom","Not until","Only then"] → ["never have i","never had i","never did i","rarely have i","rarely do you","seldom have i","seldom do you","only then did i"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu đảo ngữ (Never have I …, Rarely do …, Not until … did …)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu đảo ngữ (Never have I …, Rarely do you …, Seldom have I …, Only then did I …)"

### b2-01-3
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cách dùng | Ví dụ | Câu hỏi
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - had been + V-ing … when … | We had been digging for weeks when we found the walls. | We hadn't been digging long when …
    - Giải thích tình trạng | It had been raining all night, so we were wet. | hadn't been + V-ing
    - Câu hỏi | What had they found? | Had they been working long? – Yes, they had.
  - Sau:
    - Kéo dài rồi bị cắt ngang | We had been digging for a few weeks when we found the walls. | How long had you been digging when you found them?
    - Giải thích tình trạng | It had been raining all night, so we were wet. | Had it been raining all night?
    - Phủ định | We hadn't been digging long when we found the walls. | Had they been working long? – Yes, they had.
- **BT4 (reorder)** [We had been digging for a few weeks when we found the wall.] : nhận thêm ["When we found the wall, we had been digging for a few weeks."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["had been","'d been"] → ["had been","hadn't been","had not been","i'd been","we'd been","they'd been","he'd been","she'd been"]

### b2-01-4
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - While + quá khứ tiếp diễn | While some workers were digging, others were carrying stone. | Hai việc cùng diễn ra song song.
    - By the time + quá khứ đơn | By the time the war ended, the citadel had lost many palaces. | Mệnh đề chính dùng quá khứ hoàn thành.
    - had been + V-ing + for … | They had been building the walls for decades before … | hadn't been + V-ing
  - Sau:
    - While + QK tiếp diễn | While some workers were digging the moat, others were carrying stone. | Hai việc cùng diễn ra song song.
    - By the time + QK đơn | By the time the war ended, the citadel had lost many palaces. | Mệnh đề chính dùng quá khứ hoàn thành.
    - had been + V-ing + for … | Workers had been building the walls for decades before they finished. | Nhấn mạnh quá trình kéo dài; phủ định: hadn't been + V-ing.
- **BT4 (reorder)** [Workers had been rebuilding it for several years.] : nhận thêm ["For several years workers had been rebuilding it."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["had been","had ","'d been"] → ["had been","hadn't been","had not been","i'd been","we'd been","they'd been","he'd been","she'd been"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu quá khứ hoàn thành (tiếp diễn): had + V3 / had been + V-ing" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu quá khứ hoàn thành tiếp diễn (had been + V-ing)"
- **Bài viết mẫu:**
  - Trước: The One Pillar Pagoda in Hanoi was first built in 1049 under the Ly dynasty. According to the story, the king had dreamed of a goddess sitting on a lotus. Over the centuries, the pagoda was repaired many times. In 1954, French soldiers destroyed it as they were leaving the city. By the next year, it had been rebuilt.
  - Sau: The One Pillar Pagoda in Hanoi was first built in 1049 under the Ly dynasty. According to the story, the king had dreamed of a goddess sitting on a lotus. Over the centuries, the pagoda was repaired many times. In 1954, French soldiers destroyed it as they were leaving the city. Workers had been rebuilding it for several months before it opened again in 1955.

### b2-01-5
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Không nói
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Seldom / Rarely / Never before | + had / has / did + chủ ngữ + V: Seldom had any army moved … | Bị động: Never before had bicycles been used …
    - Only after … | Only after this defeat did France agree … | Đảo ngữ ở mệnh đề chính.
    - Not until … | Not until I read the letters did I understand … | Không viết: Not until I read the letters I understood.
  - Sau:
    - Seldom / Rarely + had | Seldom had any army moved heavy guns across such steep passes. | Seldom any army had moved heavy guns across such steep passes.
    - Never before (bị động) | Never before had bicycles been used like this. | Never before bicycles had been used like this.
    - Only after … | Only after this defeat did France agree to leave. | Only after this defeat France agreed to leave.
    - Not until … | Not until I read the letters did I understand. | Not until I read the letters I understood.
- **BT1 (fill)** : câu "Never before ___ bicycles been used like this in a war." → "Never before ___ bicycles been used like this, so the French were not ready for it."
- **BT3 (fix)** [Not until I read the letters did I understand how hard it was.] : đề "Câu sau có một lỗi. Hãy viết lại cho đúng." → "Câu sau có một lỗi đảo ngữ. Hãy viết lại cho đúng, vẫn bắt đầu bằng "Not until"."
- **BT4 (reorder)** [Seldom had an army pulled guns across such steep passes.] : đề "Sắp xếp các từ thành câu đúng." → "Sắp xếp thành câu đảo ngữ, bắt đầu bằng "Seldom"."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Never before","Never have","Never had","Rarely","Seldom","Only after","Not until"] → ["never before had","never before has","never before have","never had i","never have i","rarely has one","rarely has a","seldom had any"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu đảo ngữ (Never before had …, Rarely has …, Only after … did …)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu đảo ngữ (Never before had …, Never have I …, Rarely has one …)"

### b2-01-6
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cách dùng | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Nguyên nhân kéo dài | He had been collecting heavy taxes for years, and families were hungry. | hadn't been + V-ing
    - Bối cảnh + sự việc | While the soldiers were marching, more people joined them. | wasn't / weren't + V-ing
    - Kết quả đã xong | Soon they had won back sixty-five towns. | hadn't + V3
  - Sau:
    - Nguyên nhân kéo dài | He had been collecting heavy taxes for years, and families were hungry. | The people hadn't been living in peace for a long time.
    - Bối cảnh + sự việc | While the soldiers were marching, more people joined them. | The sisters weren't fighting alone.
    - Kết quả đã xong | Soon they had won back sixty-five towns. | The Han army hadn't expected such a strong attack.
- **grammarStep.explanationVi:** "Nguyên nhân kéo dài trước tình trạng trong quá khứ (were hungry): had been taking." → "Nguyên nhân kéo dài trước tình trạng trong quá khứ (were hungry): had been collecting."
- **Noticing:** [{"sentence":"He had been collecting heavy taxes from the people for years, and many families were hungry.\"","highlights":["had been taking","were hungry"]},{"sentence":"She and her sister had been preparing for a long time.","highlights":["had been preparing"]},{"sentence":"While the soldiers were marching from village to village, more and more people joined them.","highlights":["were marching","joined"]},{"sentence":"Soon they had won back sixty-five towns, and To Dinh had run away to China.\"","highlights":["had won back","had run away"]}] → [{"sentence":"He had been collecting heavy taxes from the people for years, and many families were hungry.\"","highlights":["had been collecting","were hungry"]},{"sentence":"She and her sister had been preparing for a long time.","highlights":["had been preparing"]},{"sentence":"While the soldiers were marching from village to village, more and more people joined them.","highlights":["were marching","joined"]},{"sentence":"Soon they had won back sixty-five towns, and To Dinh had run away to China.\"","highlights":["had won back","had run away"]}]
- **BT3 (fix)** [The governor had been collecting heavy taxes for years.] : giải thích "Sau had been phải là V-ing: taking." → "Sau had been phải là V-ing: collecting."
- **BT4 (reorder)** [They had been training with weapons for months.] : nhận thêm ["For months they had been training with weapons."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["had been","'d been"] → ["had been","hadn't been","had not been","i'd been","we'd been","they'd been","he'd been","she'd been"]
- **Bài viết mẫu:**
  - Trước: Lady Trieu was a young woman who lived in the third century. She had been living in Thanh Hoa with her brother and had been training in the mountains for years. In 248, she led an army against the Wu rulers. While she was fighting, she became a symbol of courage. People still remember her today.
  - Sau: Lady Trieu was a young woman who lived in the third century. She had been living in Thanh Hoa with her brother and had been training in the mountains for years. In 248, she led an army against the Wu rulers. She had been fighting the Wu army for months before she was defeated, and she became a symbol of courage. People still remember her today.

### b2-01-7
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Không nói
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Never / Seldom + HTHT | Never have I seen … / Seldom have I felt … | have / has + chủ ngữ + V3
    - Rarely / Only now + hiện tại đơn | Rarely do you see … / Only now do I understand … | do / does + chủ ngữ + V
    - Not only … but also | Not only did they copy …, but they also created … | Chỉ đảo ngữ ở vế Not only.
  - Sau:
    - Never / Seldom + have + S + V3 | Never have I seen such detailed carving in stone. | Never I have seen such detailed carving in stone.
    - Rarely / Only now + do + S + V | Rarely do you see such fine work. | Rarely you see such fine work.
    - Not only + did + S + V | Not only did they copy Indian styles, but they also created their own. | Not only they copied Indian styles, but they also created their own.
- **BT1 (fill)** : nhận thêm ["had"]; giải thích "Seldom + have + I + felt." → "Seldom + have / had + I + felt (đảo ngữ hiện tại hoàn thành hoặc quá khứ hoàn thành)."
- **BT2 (choice)** : phương án ["do","did","have","am"] → ["do","does","have","am"]
- **BT3 (fix)** [Never have I seen such detailed carving.] : đề "Câu sau có một lỗi. Hãy viết lại cho đúng." → "Câu sau có một lỗi đảo ngữ. Hãy viết lại cho đúng, vẫn bắt đầu bằng "Never"."
- **BT4 (reorder)** [Rarely do you see such fine work.] : đề "Sắp xếp các từ thành câu đúng." → "Sắp xếp thành câu đảo ngữ, bắt đầu bằng "Rarely"."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Never have","Never had","Rarely","Seldom","Not only","Only now","Only then","Not until"] → ["never have i","rarely do you","rarely have i","seldom have i","not only are","not only is","not only did","only now do"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu đảo ngữ (Never have I …, Rarely do you …, Not only … but also …)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu đảo ngữ (Never have I …, Rarely do you …, Not only are / did …, Only now do …)"

### b2-01-8
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Dấu hiệu
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - had been + V-ing … before | Traders had been sailing along this coast long before that. | hadn't been + V-ing
    - By + mốc thời gian | By the nineteenth century, the river had become too shallow. | hadn't + V3
    - While + quá khứ tiếp diễn | While they were waiting, they built houses. | wasn't / weren't + V-ing
  - Sau:
    - had been + V-ing | Merchants had been moving their business to Da Nang for years. | for years, long before that
    - By … + had + V3 | By the nineteenth century, the river had become too shallow. | by + mốc thời gian, by then
    - While + QK tiếp diễn | While they were waiting, they lived in Hoi An. | while, when (việc làm nền)
- **BT4 (reorder)** [Traders had been sailing along this coast for centuries.] : nhận thêm ["For centuries traders had been sailing along this coast."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["had been","had ","'d been"] → ["had been","hadn't been","had not been","i'd been","we'd been","they'd been","he'd been","she'd been"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu quá khứ hoàn thành (tiếp diễn): had + V3 / had been + V-ing" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu quá khứ hoàn thành tiếp diễn (had been + V-ing)"

### b2-02-1
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Mệnh đề | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Mệnh đề If | If + had + V3: If the city had built those drains, … | If people hadn't thrown rubbish, …
    - Mệnh đề chính | would have + V3: … we would have saved much more. | wouldn't have + V3: … the streets wouldn't have flooded.
    - Biến thể | could have / might have + V3 | If they had kept the ponds, the water might have gone down.
  - Sau:
    - If + had + V3 | If the city had built those drains in 2015, … | If people hadn't thrown rubbish into the drains, …
    - would have + V3 | … we would have saved much more. | … the streets wouldn't have flooded so badly.
    - could / might have + V3 | If they had kept the ponds, the water might have gone down faster. | … families might not have lost their furniture.
- **BT1 (fill)** : nhận thêm ["could","might"]; giải thích "Mệnh đề chính loại 3: would have + V3." → "Mệnh đề chính loại 3: would / could / might have + V3."
- **BT4 (reorder)** [If we had spent the money earlier, we would have saved more.] : nhận thêm ["We would have saved more if we had spent the money earlier."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["would have","wouldn't have","could have","might have"] → ["would have","wouldn't have","would not have","if we hadn't","if they hadn't","if i hadn't","if it hadn't","if he hadn't"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu điều kiện loại 3 (If … had + V3, … would have + V3)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu điều kiện loại 3 (If … had / hadn't + V3, … would have + V3)"

### b2-02-2
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Loại | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Quá khứ → hiện tại | If + had + V3, would + V: If they had kept the trees, we would have shade today. | If the city had invested earlier, we wouldn't have so many motorbikes now.
    - Hiện tại → quá khứ | If + quá khứ đơn, would have + V3: If I were braver, I would have said yes. | Đặc điểm hiện tại ảnh hưởng việc đã qua.
    - So với loại 3 | If we had made this video five years ago, nobody would have watched it. | Cả hai vế đều ở quá khứ.
  - Sau:
    - Quá khứ → hiện tại | If the city had invested in public transport, we wouldn't have so many motorbikes now. | If + had + V3, would + V; vế sau có now, today
    - Hiện tại → quá khứ | If I were braver, I would have said yes. | If + quá khứ đơn, would have + V3: đặc điểm hiện tại ảnh hưởng việc đã qua
    - So với loại 3 | If we had made this video five years ago, nobody would have watched it. | Cả hai vế đều ở quá khứ.
- **BT1 (fill)** : nhận thêm ["might","could"]; giải thích "Kết quả ở hiện tại (today): would + V." → "Kết quả ở hiện tại (today): would / might / could + V."
- **BT4 (reorder)** [If the city had started earlier, the air would be cleaner now.] : nhận thêm ["The air would be cleaner now if the city had started earlier.","If the city had started earlier, the air would now be cleaner."]
- **Dịch T1** [If the city had invested earlier, there would be fewer motorbikes now.]: nhận thêm ["If the city had invested earlier, now there would be fewer motorbikes."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["would be","would have","wouldn't","would "] → ["would be","wouldn't be","would not be","would now","would still","if we hadn't","if they hadn't","if i hadn't"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu điều kiện hỗn hợp (If … had + V3, … would + V … now)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu điều kiện hỗn hợp (If … had / hadn't + V3, … would be … now)"
- **Bài viết mẫu:**
  - Trước: Twenty years ago, my town decided to build a big shopping centre instead of a park. If they had built the park, children would have a safe place to play today. The air would also be cleaner. My parents say they should have complained at that time. If more people had spoken up, the town would look very different now.
  - Sau: Twenty years ago, my town decided to build a big shopping centre instead of a park. If they had built the park, children would have a safe place to play today. The air would be cleaner too. My parents say they should have complained at that time. If more people had spoken up, the town would look very different now.

### b2-02-3
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Nghĩa
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - must / might / can't have + V3 | Suy đoán: must have (chắc chắn), might have (có thể), can't have (chắc chắn không) | They can't have expected the centres to close.
    - should / ought to have + V3 | Lẽ ra nên làm mà đã không làm: They should have started earlier. | shouldn't have + V3: lẽ ra không nên làm
    - could have + V3 | Có khả năng mà không dùng: they could have used social media | couldn't have + V3: không thể nào đã …
  - Sau:
    - must have + V3 | The ones that closed must have had very little savings. | chắc hẳn đã
    - might / can't have + V3 | They can't have expected the centres to close for so long. | có thể đã / chắc chắn đã không
    - should / ought to have + V3 | They should have started delivery services much earlier. | lẽ ra nên mà không làm; shouldn't have = lẽ ra không nên
    - could have + V3 | They could have used social media. | có thể làm mà đã không làm; couldn't have = không thể nào đã
- **BT1 (fill)** : nhận thêm ["could"]; giải thích "Lẽ ra nên làm mà không làm: should have + V3." → "Lẽ ra nên làm (should) hoặc lẽ ra đã có thể làm (could) mà không làm: should / could have + V3."

### b2-02-4
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - If + had (not) + V3 | If we hadn't stopped the logging, … | If the hills had been bare, …
    - would have + V3 | … the same thing would have happened to us. | … the villages wouldn't have stayed dry.
    - Without + danh từ | Without them, the rain would have washed the soil away. | = If they hadn't been there, …
  - Sau:
    - If + had (not) + V3 | If the hills had been bare, … | If we hadn't stopped the logging here, …
    - would have + V3 | … the water would have been brown with mud. | … the villages below wouldn't have stayed dry.
    - Without + danh từ (= If … hadn't been) | Without the tree roots, the rain would have washed the soil away. | Without the new law, the forest wouldn't have survived.
- **BT1 (fill)** : nhận thêm ["might","could"]; giải thích "Mệnh đề chính loại 3: would have + V3." → "Mệnh đề chính loại 3: would / might / could have + V3."
- **BT4 (reorder)** [If the hills had been bare, the water would have been muddy.] : nhận thêm ["The water would have been muddy if the hills had been bare."]
- **Dịch T2** [They must have lost a lot of income.]: thẻ nhiễu ["should","lose","money"] → ["should","lose","little"]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["would have","wouldn't have","should have","could have"] → ["would have","wouldn't have","would not have","if we hadn't","if they hadn't","if i hadn't","if it hadn't","if he hadn't"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu điều kiện loại 3 (If … had + V3, … would have + V3)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu điều kiện loại 3 (If … had / hadn't + V3, … would have + V3)"

### b2-02-5
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Quá khứ → hiện tại | If they had continued, the community would be different now. | If the budget hadn't gone to cities, rural schools wouldn't be so poor today.
    - would be + V-ing | Kết quả đang diễn ra: … would be reading much better today. | wouldn't be + V-ing
    - So với loại 3 | If I had been in charge, I would have made a different choice. | Kết quả cũng nằm trong quá khứ.
  - Sau:
    - Quá khứ → hiện tại | If they had continued the classes, the community would be different now. | If the budget hadn't gone to cities, rural schools wouldn't be so poor today.
    - would be + V-ing | … children would be reading much better today. | … children wouldn't be struggling with reading now.
    - Loại 3: kết quả ở quá khứ | If I had been in charge, I would have made a different choice. | If I had been in charge, I wouldn't have cut the classes.
- **BT1 (fill)** : nhận thêm ["might"]; giải thích "Kết quả ở hiện tại (now): would + V." → "Kết quả ở hiện tại (now): would / might + V."
- **BT4 (reorder)** [If they had continued longer, the community would be different now.] : nhận thêm ["The community would be different now if they had continued longer.","If they had continued longer, the community would now be different."]
- **Dịch T2** [The budget went to the cities.]: thẻ nhiễu ["goes","villages","money"] → ["goes","villages","schools"]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["would be","would have","wouldn't","would "] → ["would be","wouldn't be","would not be","would now","would still","if we hadn't","if they hadn't","if i hadn't"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu điều kiện hỗn hợp (If … had + V3, … would + V … now)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu điều kiện hỗn hợp (If … had / hadn't + V3, … would be … now)"

### b2-02-6
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - must / might / can't have + V3 | The transformer must have been running too hot. | That can't have been bad luck.
    - should have + V3 | Someone should have checked the records. | Bị động: The generator should have been tested.
    - could / might have + V3 | They might have replaced it already. | Câu hỏi: Could the workers have done anything?
  - Sau:
    - must have + V3 | The transformer must have been running too hot for weeks. | gần như chắc chắn
    - can't / couldn't have + V3 | That can't have been bad luck. | chắc chắn không; không dùng mustn't have
    - should have (been) + V3 | The generator should have been tested every month. | lẽ ra nên; bị động: should have been + V3
    - might / could have + V3 | They might have replaced it already. | có thể đã; hỏi: Could they have done anything?
- **BT4 (reorder)** [The transformer must have been running too hot for weeks.] : nhận thêm ["For weeks the transformer must have been running too hot."]

### b2-02-7
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Ý nghĩa
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - would have + V3 | Kết quả chắc chắn: … I would have supported the ban. | wouldn't have + V3
    - might / could have + V3 | Kết quả có thể: … they might have changed their minds. | might not / couldn't have + V3
    - Hỗn hợp (so sánh) | If we had followed them then, we would be using less plastic today. | Kết quả ở hiện tại → would + V
  - Sau:
    - would (not) have + V3 | If we had banned free plastic bags five years ago, our customers would have changed their habits. | kết quả chắc chắn
    - might / could (not) have + V3 | If they had seen the research, they might have changed their minds. | kết quả có thể
    - Hỗn hợp (so sánh) | If we had followed them then, we would be using less plastic today. | kết quả ở hiện tại → would + V
- **BT4 (reorder)** [If I had known that, I would have supported the ban earlier.] : nhận thêm ["I would have supported the ban earlier if I had known that."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["would have","wouldn't have","might have","could have"] → ["would have","wouldn't have","would not have","if we hadn't","if they hadn't","if i hadn't","if it hadn't","if he hadn't"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu điều kiện loại 3 (If … had + V3, … would / might have + V3)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu điều kiện loại 3 (If … had / hadn't + V3, … would have + V3)"

### b2-02-8
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - should have + V3 | The police should have closed the streets earlier. | They shouldn't have waited until six.
    - could have + V3 | The water company could have warned us. | (có thể làm nhưng đã không làm)
    - must / might have + V3 | They must have known since the afternoon. | They might have stopped because of the rain.
  - Sau:
    - should have + V3 | The police should have closed the streets earlier. | They shouldn't have waited until six.
    - could have + V3 (có thể mà không làm) | The water company could have warned us. | The police couldn't have stopped the rain.
    - must / can't have + V3 | They must have known since the afternoon. | The drivers can't have known about the broken pipe.
- **BT1 (fill)** : nhận thêm ["could"]; giải thích "Lẽ ra nên làm: should have + V3." → "Lẽ ra nên làm (should) hoặc lẽ ra đã có thể làm (could): should / could have + V3."

### b2-03-1
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Thì | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Tiếp diễn | am / is / are being + V3: The script is being checked. | isn't / aren't being + V3
    - Hoàn thành | has / have been + V3: The footage has been sent. | hasn't been + V3; quá khứ: hadn't been tested
    - Tương lai và modal | will be / must be / can be + V3: Only two will be used. | won't be / mustn't be + V3
  - Sau:
    - Tiếp diễn (being + V3) | The script is being checked by the legal team. | The old map isn't being used.
    - Hoàn thành (been + V3) | The video footage has already been sent. | The cause hasn't been confirmed yet.
    - QK hoàn thành (had been + V3) | The market had been redesigned the year before. | The fire alarms hadn't been tested for a year.
    - Tương lai / modal (be + V3) | Only two photos will be used. | The cable mustn't be reported as the cause.
- **Dịch T2** [The police are investigating the cause.]: thẻ nhiễu ["is","investigated","reason"] → ["is","investigated","result"]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["is being","are being","has been","have been","will be","must be","can be"] → ["is being","are being","was being","were being","has been","have been","had been"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu bị động (is being / has been / will be + V3)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu bị động tiếp diễn hoặc hoàn thành (is / are being + V3, has / have been + V3)"

### b2-03-2
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Loại | Câu tường thuật | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Câu hỏi Wh- | asked + từ để hỏi + chủ ngữ + động từ: asked how many customers had become ill | Không đảo ngữ, không dùng do / did.
    - Câu hỏi Yes / No | asked if / whether + chủ ngữ + động từ: asked if customers would get their money back | Lùi thì: will → would, can → could.
    - Mệnh lệnh, yêu cầu | told / asked + người + to + V: told her to stay calm | not to + V: not to argue with anyone
  - Sau:
    - Câu hỏi Wh- | The first reporter asked how many customers had become ill. | Không đảo ngữ, không dùng do / did.
    - Câu hỏi Yes / No | One reporter asked if customers would get their money back. | if / whether; lùi thì: will → would, can → could
    - Mệnh lệnh, yêu cầu | I told her to stay calm and not to argue with anyone. | told / asked + người + (not) to + V
- **BT3 (fix)** [He asked me what we would do next.] : câu "He asked me what will we do next." → "He asked me what would we do next."; giải thích "Trật tự câu kể (we would) và lùi thì will → would." → "Câu tường thuật dùng trật tự câu kể: what we would do, không đảo would lên trước chủ ngữ."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["asked if","asked whether","asked what","asked how","asked why","asked when","told me to","told them to","told us to","not to"] → ["asked if","asked whether","asked me if","asked me whether","asked what","asked why","told me to","told us to"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu tường thuật câu hỏi hoặc mệnh lệnh (asked if / what …, told … to …)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu tường thuật câu hỏi hoặc mệnh lệnh (asked if / whether / what / why …, told me / us to …)"

### b2-03-3
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Động từ | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - admit / deny / suggest + V-ing | She admitted making a mistake. / She denied having problems. | deny + V-ing = nói mình không làm
    - promise / refuse / offer + to + V | She promised to release a new song. / She refused to answer. | refuse to + V = từ chối làm
    - tell / ask + người + (not) to + V | She told me to wait and see. | He asked me not to publish the title.
  - Sau:
    - admit / deny / suggest + V-ing | She admitted making a mistake. / She denied having problems. | deny + V-ing = nói mình không làm
    - promise / refuse / offer + to + V | She promised to release a new song. / She refused to answer. | refuse to + V = từ chối làm
    - tell / ask + người + (not) to + V | He asked me not to publish the title. | not đứng trước to: told me not to + V
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["admitted","denied","promised","refused","offered","suggested","warned","advised"] → ["admitted","denied","promised to","promised not","refused to","offered to","suggested"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 động từ tường thuật (admitted, denied, promised, refused, offered …)" → "Có ít nhất 1 động từ tường thuật (admitted, denied, promised to, refused to, offered to, suggested …)"

### b2-03-4
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Thì | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Hiện tại tiếp diễn | is / are being + V3: The angles are being adjusted. | isn't / aren't being + V3
    - Hiện tại hoàn thành | has / have been + V3: A bridge has been closed. | hasn't / haven't been + V3
    - Modal / tương lai | must / has to / will be + V3: The headlines must be changed. | won't be / can't be + V3
  - Sau:
    - Hiện tại tiếp diễn | The camera angles are being adjusted. | The old graphics aren't being used tonight.
    - Hiện tại hoàn thành | A bridge has been closed in the north of the city. | The headlines haven't been changed yet.
    - Modal / tương lai | The show will be presented by Huong. | The cameras can't be left at the usual height.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["is being","are being","has been","have been","will be","must be","has to be"] → ["is being","are being","was being","were being","has been","have been","had been"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu bị động (is being / has been / will be / must be + V3)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu bị động tiếp diễn hoặc hoàn thành (is / are being + V3, has / have been + V3)"

### b2-03-5
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Loại | Câu tường thuật | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Câu hỏi Wh- | asked (him) + when / why / how + chủ ngữ + động từ | asked him why ticket prices were going up
    - Câu hỏi Yes / No | asked (me) if / whether + chủ ngữ + động từ | asked me if I had taken a city bus
    - Mệnh lệnh | told me to + V: told me to focus on the new routes | told me not to + V: told me not to write …
  - Sau:
    - Câu hỏi Wh- | I asked him when the new bus routes would start. | asked + từ để hỏi + S + V, không đảo ngữ
    - Câu hỏi Yes / No | He asked me if I had taken a city bus. | asked (me) if / whether + S + V
    - Mệnh lệnh | He told me to focus on the new routes. | phủ định: told me not to + V
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["asked if","asked whether","asked me","asked him","asked her","told me to","told me not to","not to"] → ["asked if","asked whether","asked me if","asked me whether","asked me why","asked me how","told me to","told me not to"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu tường thuật câu hỏi hoặc mệnh lệnh (asked if / why …, told me to …)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu tường thuật câu hỏi hoặc mệnh lệnh (asked (me) if / whether / why …, told me (not) to …)"

### b2-03-6
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Động từ | Ví dụ | Không nói
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - suggest / recommend + V-ing | I suggest releasing a statement. / I recommend inviting journalists. | Không viết: suggest to release
    - advise / warn + người + (not) to + V | I'd advise you not to attack anyone. | She warned me not to make the story bigger.
    - admit / deny / promise | Admit that it is serious. / Deny the claim. | promise + to + V: promise to keep them informed
  - Sau:
    - suggest / recommend + V-ing | I suggest releasing an official statement. | I suggest to release an official statement.
    - advise / warn + người + (not) to | I'd advise you not to attack anyone. | I'd advise you to not attack anyone.
    - deny / admit + V-ing / that | The company denied using river water. | The company denied to use river water.
    - promise + to + V | We promise to keep you informed. | We promise keeping you informed.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["suggest","recommend","advise","warn","promise","deny","admit"] → ["i suggest","suggest that","recommend","advise you","i'd advise","warn you","i'd warn"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 động từ tường thuật hoặc khuyên (suggest, recommend, advise, warn …)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu khuyên / đề nghị (I suggest …, I recommend …, I'd advise you (not) to …, I'd warn you …)"

### b2-03-7
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Thì | Khẳng định | Câu hỏi
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Hiện tại hoàn thành | has / have (just) been + V3: The copies have just been printed. | Câu hỏi: Has the front page been printed yet?
    - Tiếp diễn | is / are being + V3: The routes are being finalised. | isn't / aren't being + V3
    - Tương lai / modal | will be + V3: The papers will be delivered before dawn. | can be + V3: so much paper can be printed
  - Sau:
    - Hiện tại hoàn thành | The first copies have just been printed. | Has the front page been printed yet?
    - Hiện tại tiếp diễn | The delivery routes are being finalised. | Are the routes being finalised now?
    - Tương lai / modal | The papers will be delivered before dawn. | Will the papers be delivered on time?
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["is being","are being","has been","have been","will be","are checked","is printed"] → ["is being","are being","was being","has been","have been","are checked","is printed","are printed"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu bị động (is being / has been / will be + V3)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu bị động (is / are being + V3, has / have been + V3, are checked, is printed …)"

### b2-03-8
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Loại | Câu tường thuật | Không nói
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - asked + từ để hỏi | He asked how long it would take us to publish the story. | Không đảo ngữ: không viết how long would it take
    - asked + whether / if | He asked me whether we could protect his identity. | Lùi thì: can → could, will → would
    - asked / told + người + (not) to | He told me to call him only from a new number. | He asked me not to use his name.
  - Sau:
    - asked + từ để hỏi | He asked how long it would take us to publish the story. | He asked how long would it take us to publish the story.
    - asked + whether / if (lùi thì) | He asked me whether we could protect his identity. | He asked me could we protect his identity.
    - asked / told + người + (not) to | He asked me not to use his name. | He asked me to not use his name.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["asked if","asked whether","asked how","asked why","asked me","told me to","not to"] → ["asked if","asked whether","asked me if","asked me whether","asked how","asked me not to","told me to","told me not to"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu tường thuật câu hỏi hoặc mệnh lệnh (asked whether / how …, told me to …)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu tường thuật câu hỏi hoặc mệnh lệnh (asked (me) whether / how …, asked / told me (not) to …)"

### b2-04-1
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Từ nối | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - as long as / provided (that) | Điều kiện bắt buộc: We'll sign as long as the points are clear. | = chỉ khi, với điều kiện là
    - in case | Phòng xa: I'll send it early in case your lawyer wants to read it. | in case ≠ if: làm trước để đề phòng
    - Thì sau các từ này | Hiện tại đơn: provided that it doesn't change | Không dùng will sau in case / as long as
  - Sau:
    - as long as / provided (that) | We'll sign this week as long as a few points are clear. | = chỉ khi, với điều kiện là
    - in case | I'll send it early in case your lawyer wants to read it. | làm trước để đề phòng (≠ if)
    - Thì sau từ nối | We'll accept the price provided that it doesn't change. | hiện tại đơn, không dùng will
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["as long as","provided that","provided ","in case"] → ["provided that","providing that","as long as","so long as","in case"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu với as long as / provided that / in case" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu với provided that / as long as / in case"

### b2-04-2
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Thì | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Tương lai tiếp diễn | will be + V-ing: In July, we will be testing the app. | won't be + V-ing
    - Tương lai hoàn thành | will have + V3: By the end of August, we will have solved them. | won't have + V3
    - Dấu hiệu | Tiếp diễn: in July, at this time next week … | Hoàn thành: by + mốc (by Christmas, by the end of …)
  - Sau:
    - Tương lai tiếp diễn | In July, we will be testing the new app with five hundred customers. | We won't be testing it with all our customers yet.
    - Tương lai hoàn thành | By the end of September, we will have solved these problems. | By then, we won't have moved every customer to the new app.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["will be","will have","'ll be","'ll have"] → ["will be working","will be starting","will be meeting","will be using","will have finished","will have found","will have started","'ll have finished"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu tương lai tiếp diễn hoặc hoàn thành (will be + V-ing / will have + V3)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu tương lai tiếp diễn hoặc hoàn thành (vd. will be working, will have finished)"

### b2-04-3
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - be supposed to + V | Quy định, việc được mong đợi: You're supposed to write a short report. | not supposed to: không được làm (theo quy định)
    - be allowed to + V | Được phép: They're allowed to wait in the café. | Visitors aren't allowed to go upstairs alone.
    - needn't + V | Không cần thiết: You needn't give us the original. | needn't ≠ mustn't (mustn't = bị cấm)
  - Sau:
    - be supposed to + V | You're supposed to write a short report every day. | theo quy định; not supposed to = không được làm
    - be allowed to + V | Visitors are allowed to wait in the café. | được phép; aren't allowed to = bị cấm
    - needn't + V | You needn't give us the original. | không cần; ≠ mustn't (bị cấm)
- **BT1 (fill)** : đề "Điền một từ vào chỗ trống." → "Điền một từ mang nghĩa 'không cần'."; nhận thêm ["need not"]
- **Dịch T4** [You can eat in the kitchen, provided that you clean up afterwards.]: nhận thêm ["Provided you clean up afterwards, you can eat in the kitchen."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["supposed to","allowed to","needn't"] → ["supposed to","allowed to","needn't","need not"]

### b2-04-4
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Từ nối | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - provided (that) | Điều kiện trang trọng: We'll sign provided that your prices stay the same. | Hay dùng trong hợp đồng, đàm phán
    - as long as | Điều kiện (thân mật hơn): fixed prices as long as there are no storms | = miễn là
    - in case | Phòng xa: keep frozen vegetables in case a delivery is late | Không viết: in case it will be late
  - Sau:
    - provided (that) | We're happy to sign provided that your prices stay the same. | trang trọng, hay dùng trong hợp đồng
    - as long as | The prices will stay fixed as long as there are no storms. | = miễn là; thân mật hơn
    - in case | Keep some frozen vegetables in case a delivery is late. | đề phòng; không viết: in case it will be late
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["provided that","as long as","in case","provided "] → ["provided that","providing that","as long as","so long as","in case"]

### b2-04-5
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - will be + V-ing | This time next month, we'll be working on the report. | Câu hỏi: Will we be working at weekends?
    - will have + V3 | By then, we will have checked every number twice. | won't have + V3
    - By the time + hiện tại đơn | By the time we start, they will have reviewed the documents. | Không viết: By the time we will start
  - Sau:
    - will be + V-ing | This time next month, we'll be working on the annual budget report. | việc đang làm lúc đó; hỏi: Will we be …?
    - will have + V3 | By then, we will have checked every number twice. | xong trước mốc; phủ định: won't have + V3
    - By the time + hiện tại đơn | By the time we start, they will have reviewed the documents. | không viết: By the time we will start
- **BT2 (choice)** : câu "Duc ___ for Mai while she is away." → "Next month, Duc ___ for Mai while she is away."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["will be","will have","'ll be","'ll have"] → ["will be working","will be meeting","will be preparing","will be checking","will have finished","will have sent","will have checked","'ll have finished"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu will be + V-ing hoặc will have + V3" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu tương lai tiếp diễn hoặc hoàn thành (vd. will be working, will have finished)"

### b2-04-6
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - be supposed to | Quy định: You're supposed to reply within an hour. | Câu hỏi: Are we supposed to answer immediately?
    - be allowed to | Cho phép: They will be allowed to work from home. | not allowed to = bị cấm
    - needn't | Không cần: You needn't come in just to print documents. | = don't need to / don't have to
  - Sau:
    - be supposed to | You're supposed to reply within an hour. | theo quy định; hỏi: Are we supposed to …?
    - be allowed to | They will be allowed to work from home. | not allowed to = bị cấm
    - needn't | You needn't come in just to print documents. | = don't need to / don't have to
- **BT1 (fill)** : đề "Điền một từ vào chỗ trống." → "Điền một từ: be ___ to + V = theo quy định phải làm."; nhận thêm ["expected","required","meant"]; giải thích "Quy định: be supposed to + V." → "Quy định: be supposed to + V (cũng có thể nói be expected / required to)."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["supposed to","allowed to","needn't"] → ["supposed to","allowed to","needn't","need not"]

### b2-04-7
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Từ nối | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - provided (that) | We should go ahead, provided that we follow the rules. | Có thể đứng đầu câu: Provided that they're trained well, …
    - as long as | I'm happy to sign as long as they're for two years. | = miễn là
    - in case | We'll apply now in case it takes longer. | in case ≠ if: việc làm sau sự cố thì dùng if (If sales are slow, we could sell online).
  - Sau:
    - provided (that) | We should go ahead, provided that we follow all the local rules. | có thể đứng đầu câu: Provided that …, …
    - as long as | I'm happy to sign as long as they're for two years. | = miễn là
    - in case | We'll apply now, in case it takes longer. | đề phòng trước; việc làm sau sự cố thì dùng if: If sales are slow, …
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["provided that","as long as","in case","provided "] → ["provided that","providing that","as long as","so long as","in case"]

### b2-04-8
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - will be + V-ing | Hoạt động trong tuần: We'll be running online ads from Monday. | Hỏi lịch sự: Will you be joining us?
    - will have + V3 | Kết quả trước mốc: By Friday, we will have given away twenty thousand bottles. | won't have + V3
    - By + mốc thời gian | by Wednesday, by then, by the end of next week | Dấu hiệu của tương lai hoàn thành
  - Sau:
    - will be + V-ing | From Monday, we'll be running online ads. | việc đang diễn ra; hỏi lịch sự: Will you be joining us?
    - will have + V3 | By Friday, we will have given away twenty thousand bottles. | xong trước mốc; phủ định: won't have + V3
    - By + mốc thời gian | By Wednesday, the delivery company will have taken the posters. | by then, by the end of next week …
- **BT2 (choice)** : câu "On Tuesday, I ___ the product at a big supermarket." → "Next Tuesday, I ___ the product at a big supermarket."
- **Dịch T4** [I will be presenting the product at the big supermarket.]: thẻ nhiễu ["have","presented","small"] → ["has","presents","small"]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["will be","will have","'ll be","'ll have"] → ["will be launching","will be selling","will be working","will be posting","will have sold","will have given","will have launched","will have finished"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu will be + V-ing hoặc will have + V3" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu tương lai tiếp diễn hoặc hoàn thành (vd. will be working, will have finished)"

### b2-05-1
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Nghĩa
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - used to + V | Thói quen cũ, nay không còn: I used to get up at six. | didn't use to + V: I didn't use to cook.
    - would + V | Hành động lặp lại trong quá khứ: I would meet friends for iced coffee. | Không dùng would cho trạng thái (be, have, live …).
    - be / get used to + V-ing | Đã quen / dần quen: I'm used to drinking hot tea. / I'm getting used to the cold. | wasn't used to + V-ing / danh từ
  - Sau:
    - used to + V | I used to get up at six. / I didn't use to cook. | Thói quen cũ, nay không còn
    - would + V | I would meet friends for iced coffee. | Hành động lặp lại trong quá khứ; không dùng cho trạng thái (be, have, live …)
    - be / get used to + V-ing / danh từ | I'm used to drinking hot tea. / I'm getting used to the cold. | Đã quen / đang dần quen
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["used to","would ","getting used","got used"] → ["used to","didn't use to","getting used","got used","get used","would always","would often","would usually"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu với used to / would / be (get) used to" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu với used to / be (get) used to / would always (often, usually)"

### b2-05-2
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - wish + quá khứ đơn | Ước cho hiện tại: I wish it were cheaper. | I wish I weren't so tired.
    - wish + quá khứ hoàn thành | Tiếc về quá khứ: I wish I had studied computer science. | I wish they hadn't pushed me so hard.
    - If only … | Nhấn mạnh hơn wish: If only someone had told us! | Chia thì giống như sau wish.
  - Sau:
    - wish + quá khứ đơn (hiện tại) | I wish it were cheaper. | I wish I weren't so tired.
    - wish + had + V3 (tiếc quá khứ) | I wish I had studied computer science. | I wish they hadn't pushed me so hard.
    - If only … (chia như wish, mạnh hơn) | If only someone had told us! | If only it weren't so expensive!
- **BT1 (fill)** : đề "Điền một từ vào chỗ trống." → "Điền một từ vào chỗ trống (Ước gì bố mẹ tôi đã không ép tôi nhiều như vậy)."
- **Dịch T1** [Boring, to be honest. I check numbers all day.]: thẻ nhiễu ["bored","fun","letters"] → ["exciting","fun","letters"]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["I wish","If only","wish I","wishes"] → ["i wish","wish i","if only","wish you","wishes i","wish they","wish he","wish she"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu với wish / If only" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu với I wish / If only"

### b2-05-3
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Nghĩa
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - would rather + V | Bản thân thích hơn: I'd rather study in Sydney. | I'd rather not move to London.
    - would rather + người + quá khứ đơn | Muốn người khác làm: I'd rather you sent it this week. | I'd rather you didn't tell the others.
    - It's (high) time + quá khứ đơn | Đã đến lúc: It's high time you submitted your application. | Cũng có thể: It's time to start.
  - Sau:
    - would rather + V | I'd rather study in Sydney. / I'd rather not move to London. | Mình thích lựa chọn này hơn
    - would rather + người + quá khứ đơn | I'd rather you sent it this week. / I'd rather you didn't tell the others. | Muốn người khác làm (hoặc không làm) gì
    - It's (high) time + quá khứ đơn | It's high time you submitted your application. / It's time to start. | Đã đến lúc, đáng lẽ phải làm rồi
- **BT3 (fix)** [I'd rather study in Sydney than London.] : nhận thêm ["I'd rather study in Sydney than in London."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["would rather","'d rather","It's time","it's time","high time"] → ["would rather","'d rather","it's time","it is time","high time"]

### b2-05-4
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - used to + V | My mum used to make three meals a day. | I never used to cook. / I didn't use to cook.
    - would + V | My grandmother would bring us cakes every Sunday. | Chỉ dùng cho hành động lặp lại, không cho trạng thái.
    - be / get used to + V-ing | I'm getting used to sharing a kitchen. | I'm not used to getting up early.
  - Sau:
    - used to + V | My mum used to make three meals a day. | I never used to cook. / I didn't use to cook.
    - would + V (chỉ hành động lặp lại) | My grandmother would bring us cakes every Sunday. | She wouldn't let us help in the kitchen.
    - be / get used to + V-ing | I'm getting used to sharing a kitchen. | I'm not used to getting up early.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["used to","would ","getting used","got used"] → ["used to","didn't use to","getting used","got used","get used","would always","would often","would usually"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu với used to / would / be (get) used to" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu với used to / be (get) used to / would always (often, usually)"

### b2-05-5
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Hiện tại: wish + quá khứ đơn | I wish I were there now. | I wish I weren't so nervous.
    - Quá khứ: wish + had + V3 | I wish I had practised speaking more. | I wish I hadn't been so nervous.
    - If only | If only I had more time! (hiện tại) | If only I had known! (quá khứ)
  - Sau:
    - Hiện tại: wish + quá khứ đơn | I wish I were there now. | I wish I weren't so nervous.
    - Quá khứ: wish + had + V3 | I wish I had practised speaking more. | I wish I hadn't been so nervous.
    - If only (mạnh hơn wish) | If only I had more time! / If only I had known! | If only I weren't so shy!
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["I wish","If only","wish I","wish they"] → ["i wish","wish i","if only","wish you","wishes i","wish they","wish he","wish she"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu với wish / If only" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu với I wish / If only"

### b2-05-6
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Nghĩa
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - would rather + V (than + V) | I'd rather start there than move to Ho Chi Minh City. | I'd rather not move.
    - would rather + người + quá khứ đơn | Would you rather I checked it for you? | I'd rather you didn't wait too long.
    - It's (high) time + quá khứ đơn | It's time you sent your application. | It's high time you got started.
  - Sau:
    - would rather + V (than + V) | I'd rather start there than move to Ho Chi Minh City. / I'd rather not move. | Mình thích lựa chọn này hơn
    - would rather + người + quá khứ đơn | Would you rather I checked it for you? / I'd rather you didn't wait too long. | Muốn người khác làm (hoặc không làm) gì
    - It's (high) time + quá khứ đơn | It's time you sent your application. / It's high time you got started. | Việc cần làm ngay; high nhấn mạnh hơn
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["would rather","'d rather","It's time","it's time","high time"] → ["would rather","'d rather","it's time","it is time","high time"]

### b2-05-7
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - used to + V | In Vietnam, I used to sit quietly and take notes. | didn't use to + V
    - would + V | The teacher would talk, and we would listen. | Chỉ cho hành động lặp lại, không cho trạng thái.
    - be / get used to + V-ing / danh từ | I'm getting used to the fast debates. | I wasn't used to giving my opinion.
  - Sau:
    - used to + V | In Vietnam, I used to sit quietly and take notes. | We didn't use to question the teacher.
    - would + V (chỉ hành động lặp lại) | The teacher would talk, and we would listen. | We wouldn't ask questions in class.
    - be / get used to + V-ing / danh từ | I'm getting used to the fast debates. | I wasn't used to giving my opinion.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["used to","would ","getting used","got used"] → ["used to","didn't use to","getting used","got used","get used","would always","would often","would usually"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu với used to / would / be (get) used to" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu với used to / be (get) used to / would always (often, usually)"

### b2-05-8
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Nghĩa
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - wish + quá khứ đơn | I wish our buses were like that. | Mong hiện tại khác đi.
    - wish + had + V3 | I wish I had travelled more. | I wish I hadn't worked so hard.
    - wish + would + V | She wishes I would stop talking about work. | Phàn nàn về hành động của người khác.
  - Sau:
    - wish + quá khứ đơn | I wish our buses were like that. | Mong hiện tại khác đi
    - wish + had + V3 | I wish I had travelled more. / I wish I hadn't worked so hard. | Tiếc việc đã làm / đã không làm
    - wish + would + V | She wishes I would stop talking about work. | Phàn nàn về hành động của người khác
- **BT1 (fill)** : đề "Điền một từ vào chỗ trống." → "Điền một từ vào chỗ trống (Ước gì hồi ở Nhật tôi đã không làm việc vất vả như vậy)."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["I wish","If only","wish I","wishes"] → ["i wish","wish i","if only","wish you","wishes i","wish they","wish he","wish she"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu với wish / If only" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu với I wish / If only"

### b2-06-1
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - who (người) | Dr Nam, who grew up in a village, started the project. | Không dùng that trong mệnh đề này.
    - which (vật) | The filter, which uses energy from the sun, can clean water. | Bỏ mệnh đề đi, câu vẫn đủ nghĩa.
    - Dấu phẩy | Hai dấu phẩy khi mệnh đề ở giữa câu. | Một dấu phẩy khi mệnh đề ở cuối câu.
  - Sau:
    - who (người) | Dr Nam, who grew up in a village, started the project. | Không dùng that; ở giữa câu thì có hai dấu phẩy
    - which (vật) | The filter, which uses energy from the sun, can clean water. | Bỏ mệnh đề đi, câu vẫn đủ nghĩa
    - Ở cuối câu | The idea came from Dr Nam, who grew up in the Mekong Delta. | Chỉ một dấu phẩy, trước who / which
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** [", which",", who",", whose"] → ["which","who","whose","whom"]

### b2-06-2
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | = câu thường
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - V-ing … | Chủ động: Using the sensor, farmers reduced their fertiliser. | Chủ ngữ hai vế phải là một.
    - V3 … | Bị động: Placed too close to the canals, some sensors gave wrong readings. | = Because they were placed …
    - Having + V3 … | Việc xảy ra trước: Having completed the trials, the team met. | = After they had completed …
  - Sau:
    - V-ing (chủ động) | Using the sensor, farmers reduced their fertiliser. | = Because they used the sensor, farmers reduced …
    - V3 (bị động) | Placed too close to the canals, some sensors gave wrong readings. | = Because they were placed too close to the canals, …
    - Having + V3 (xảy ra trước) | Having completed the trials, the team met. | = After they had completed the trials, …
- **BT3 (fix)** [Placed too close to the canals, some sensors gave wrong readings.] : nhận thêm ["Being placed too close to the canals, some sensors gave wrong readings."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Having","Using","Seeing","Placed","Tested","Designed"] → ["having tested","having finished","having completed","having used","having been","having seen","having made","not having"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 mệnh đề phân từ (Using …, Having + V3 …, Tested …)" → "Có ít nhất 1 mệnh đề phân từ với Having + V3 (vd. Having finished …, …)"

### b2-06-3
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Trường hợp | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Ở giữa câu | Our software, which we call LungSee, marks problem areas. | Đặt giữa hai dấu phẩy.
    - Ở cuối câu | She presented her software, which can read X-rays. | Một dấu phẩy trước which.
    - which làm tân ngữ | Our software, which we call LungSee, … | Không được bỏ which (khác mệnh đề xác định).
  - Sau:
    - Ở giữa câu | The final decision, which is always made by a doctor, is the most important part. | Đặt giữa hai dấu phẩy
    - Ở cuối câu | She presented her software, which can read X-rays. | Một dấu phẩy trước which
    - which làm tân ngữ | Our software, which we call LungSee, marks problem areas. | Không được bỏ which (khác mệnh đề xác định)
    - who (người) | My brother, who is a computer engineer, wrote the code. | Không dùng which hay that cho người
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** [", which",", who",", whose"] → ["which","who","whose","whom"]

### b2-06-4
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - , which + động từ | Our film, which is made from cassava starch, breaks down quickly. | which làm chủ ngữ của mệnh đề.
    - , which + chủ ngữ + động từ | the cassava roots, which we buy from farmers | which làm tân ngữ, không được bỏ.
    - Cuối câu | … a little vegetable oil, which makes the film soft. | Một dấu phẩy, không dùng that.
  - Sau:
    - , which + động từ | Our film, which is made from cassava starch, breaks down quickly. | which làm chủ ngữ của mệnh đề
    - , which + chủ ngữ + động từ | We peel the cassava roots, which we buy from farmers. | which làm tân ngữ, không được bỏ
    - Cuối câu | … a little vegetable oil, which makes the film soft. | Một dấu phẩy, không dùng that
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** [", which",", who",", whose"] → ["which","who","whose","whom"]

### b2-06-5
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | = câu thường
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - V-ing (chủ động) | Using six sensors, the drone corrects its position. | = Because it uses six sensors, …
    - V3 (bị động) | Designed to watch traffic, a drone is flying above the park. | = It was designed to watch traffic.
    - Having + V3 (xảy ra trước) | Having finished its route, it's coming home. | = After it has finished its route, …
  - Sau:
    - V-ing (chủ động) | Using six sensors, the drone corrects its position. | = Because it uses six sensors, …
    - V3 (bị động) | Designed to watch traffic, a drone is flying above the park. | = It was designed to watch traffic.
    - Having + V3 (xảy ra trước) | Having finished its route, it's coming home. | = After it has finished its route, …
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Designed","Using","Having","Made","Built"] → ["having finished","having flown","having tested","having completed","having been","having made","having used","not having"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 mệnh đề phân từ (Designed …, Using …, Having + V3 …)" → "Có ít nhất 1 mệnh đề phân từ với Having + V3 (vd. Having finished …, …)"

### b2-06-6
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Thay cho
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - which chỉ danh từ | These frames, which are made from local clay, help coral grow. | which = the frames
    - which chỉ cả ý trước | Metal goes rusty in salt water, which can harm the coral. | which = việc kim loại bị gỉ
    - who chỉ người | Fishermen, who know the sea, show us the best places. | Không dùng which cho người.
  - Sau:
    - which chỉ danh từ | These frames, which are made from local clay, help coral grow. | the frames
    - which chỉ cả ý trước | Metal goes rusty in salt water, which can harm the coral. | cả việc kim loại bị gỉ
    - who chỉ người | Fishermen, who know the sea, show us the best places. | fishermen (không dùng which cho người)
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** [", which",", who",", whose"] → ["which","who","whose","whom"]

### b2-06-7
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | = câu thường
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - V-ing (chủ động) | Hoping to raise money, Hieu is talking to investors. | Using no chemicals, it's safer than sprays.
    - V3 (bị động) | Attracted by the light, they fly into the box. | Tested in two hundred homes, it reduced mosquitoes.
    - Having + V3 | Having sold five thousand units, we want to sell in supermarkets. | Việc bán đã xảy ra trước.
  - Sau:
    - V-ing (chủ động) | Hoping to raise money, Hieu is talking to investors. / Using no chemicals, it's safer than sprays. | = Because he hopes to raise money, Hieu …
    - V3 (bị động) | Attracted by the light, they fly into the box. / Tested in two hundred homes, it reduced mosquitoes. | = Because they are attracted by the light, they …
    - Having + V3 (xảy ra trước) | Having sold five thousand units, we want to sell in supermarkets. | = Because we have sold five thousand units, we …
- **BT3 (fix)** [Tested in two hundred homes, it reduced mosquitoes.] : nhận thêm ["Having been tested in two hundred homes, it reduced mosquitoes."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Using","Tested","Having","Designed","Made"] → ["having sold","having tested","having finished","having raised","having been","having made","having used","not having"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 mệnh đề phân từ (Using …, Tested …, Having + V3 …)" → "Có ít nhất 1 mệnh đề phân từ với Having + V3 (vd. Having finished …, …)"

### b2-06-8
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Trường hợp | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Ở giữa câu | The battery, which they have been developing, will be tested. | Hai dấu phẩy; động từ chính đi sau.
    - Chỉ cả ý trước | Fast charging makes the battery hot, which damages it. | which = việc pin bị nóng
    - Người | The director, who always asks about money, will want to know. | Dùng who, không dùng which hay that.

    - Người | The director, who always asks about money, will want to know. | Dùng who, không dùng which hay that.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** [", which",", who",", whose"] → ["which","who","whose","whom"]

### b2-07-1
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Từ | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - each / every + danh từ số ít | Each part has … / every bottle catches the light | Động từ chia số ít.
    - both + danh từ số nhiều | Both sculptures are made from recycled materials. | both of them + động từ số nhiều
    - either / neither (of) | Neither of them is beautiful. / Either A or B would be good. | neither đã phủ định, không thêm not
  - Sau:
    - each / every + danh từ số ít | Each part has been painted a different colour. / Every bottle catches the light. | Động từ chia số ít
    - both + danh từ số nhiều | Both sculptures are made from recycled materials. | both (of them) + động từ số nhiều
    - either / neither (of) | Neither of them is beautiful. / Either A or B would be good. | neither đã phủ định, không thêm not
- **BT1 (fill)** : đề "Điền một từ vào chỗ trống." → "Điền một từ vào chỗ trống: "Cả hai bức tượng đều làm từ vật liệu tái chế.""
- **BT3 (fix)** [Each part has been painted a different colour.] : nhận thêm ["Each of the parts has been painted a different colour."]
- **Dịch T3** [She only likes silk paintings of flowers.]: nhận thêm ["She likes only silk paintings of flowers."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["each","Each","every","Every","both","Both","either","neither","Neither"] → ["both","each","every","either","neither"]

### b2-07-2
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Từ nối | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - although / even though + mệnh đề | Although it doesn't show a real object, it can express feelings. | Không dùng although + danh từ.
    - despite / in spite of + danh từ / V-ing | despite their beauty / in spite of what you say | Không viết: despite they are beautiful
    - However, + câu mới | However, artists also need to try new things. | Thường đứng đầu câu, có dấu phẩy.
  - Sau:
    - although / even though + mệnh đề | Although it doesn't show a real object, it can express feelings. | Không dùng although + danh từ
    - despite / in spite of + danh từ / V-ing | Despite their beauty, they all look quite similar. / In spite of what you say, … | Không dùng với mệnh đề: không nói despite they are beautiful
    - However, + câu mới | However, artists also need to try new things. | Thường đứng đầu câu, có dấu phẩy
- **BT1 (fill)** : đề "Điền một từ vào chỗ trống." → "Điền một từ vào chỗ trống (nghĩa: mặc dù)."
- **BT3 (fix)** [Although it is abstract, I like it.] : nhận thêm ["Even though it is abstract, I like it.","Though it is abstract, I like it.","Despite being abstract, I like it.","Despite it being abstract, I like it.","In spite of being abstract, I like it.","In spite of it being abstract, I like it."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Although","although","However","however","despite","Despite","in spite of","In spite of","even though"] → ["although","though","however","despite","in spite of"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 từ nối although / however / despite / in spite of" → "Có ít nhất 1 từ nối although / though / however / despite / in spite of"

### b2-07-3
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Từ | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - either / neither + danh từ số ít | either show is a good choice / Neither of them has disappointed | Động từ chia số ít.
    - both … and / either … or / neither … nor | Both the musicians and the puppeteers deserve praise. | Không trộn: both … or, either … and
    - each / every | Each show has its own character. / every scene is perfectly timed | each: từng cái riêng; every: tất cả
  - Sau:

    - both … and / either … or / neither … nor | Both the musicians and the puppeteers deserve praise. | Không trộn: both … or, either … and
    - each / every | Each show has its own character. / Every scene is perfectly timed. | each: từng cái riêng; every: tất cả
- **BT3 (fix)** [Both the musicians and the puppeteers deserve praise.] : nhận thêm ["Either the musicians or the puppeteers deserve praise."]

### b2-07-4
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Từ nối | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - although / even though + mệnh đề | Even though some people complain, the murals are good for business. | even though nhấn mạnh hơn although
    - despite / in spite of + danh từ | In spite of my doubts, … / despite my age, … | Despite that, … (that = điều vừa nói)
    - However / Nevertheless / On the other hand | Nevertheless, the street looks so much better now. | Đứng đầu câu, sau đó có dấu phẩy.
  - Sau:
    - although / even though + mệnh đề | Even though some people complain, the murals are good for business. | even though nhấn mạnh hơn although
    - despite / in spite of + danh từ | Despite my age, I can still change my mind. / In spite of my doubts, … | Despite that, … = dù vậy (that = điều vừa nói)
    - However / Nevertheless / On the other hand | Nevertheless, the street looks so much better now. | Đứng đầu câu, sau đó có dấu phẩy
- **BT1 (fill)** : đề "Điền một từ vào chỗ trống." → "Điền một từ vào chỗ trống (nghĩa: mặc dù)."
- **BT3 (fix)** [Despite my doubts, I have to admit it looks better.] : nhận thêm ["In spite of my doubts, I have to admit it looks better.","Although I have doubts, I have to admit it looks better.","Even though I have doubts, I have to admit it looks better.","Though I have doubts, I have to admit it looks better."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Although","although","However","Despite","despite","Nevertheless","On the other hand","Even though","even though","In spite of"] → ["although","though","despite","in spite of","however","nevertheless","on the other hand"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 từ nối although / despite / however / nevertheless / on the other hand" → "Có ít nhất 1 từ nối although / despite / in spite of / however / nevertheless / on the other hand"

### b2-07-5
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Từ | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - each / every + số ít | Each piece is made from wood. / Every photo shows daily life. | Every time we go there, …
    - either / neither / both of + us / them | Either of them would give us ideas. / Neither of us has much time. | both of us + động từ số nhiều
    - either … or … | We can either have brunch first or go straight there. | Hai lựa chọn, chọn một.

    - either / neither / both of + us / them | Either of them would give us ideas. / Neither of us has much time. | neither of + động từ số ít; both of us + số nhiều
    - either … or … | We can either have brunch first or go straight there. | Hai lựa chọn, chọn một

### b2-07-6
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Từ nối | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - although / even though + mệnh đề | Even though I love it, I had to teach maths. | Có thể đứng đầu hoặc giữa câu.
    - despite / in spite of + danh từ | In spite of my age, I don't mind it. | Despite that = dù vậy
    - On the one hand … On the other hand … | On the one hand, we lose the tradition. On the other hand, it will die. | Trình bày hai mặt của vấn đề.
  - Sau:
    - although / even though + mệnh đề | Even though I love it, I had to teach maths. | Đứng đầu hoặc giữa câu; không thêm but
    - despite / in spite of + danh từ | In spite of my age, I don't mind it. | Despite that = dù vậy
    - On the one hand … On the other hand … | On the one hand, we lose the tradition. On the other hand, it will die. | Trình bày hai mặt của vấn đề
- **BT1 (fill)** : nhận thêm ["Though","While","Whilst"]; giải thích "Trước mệnh đề: Although." → "Trước mệnh đề: Although (hoặc Though / While)."
- **BT3 (fix)** [Although it is famous, few people know it.] : nhận thêm ["It is famous, but few people know it.","Even though it is famous, few people know it.","Though it is famous, few people know it."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Although","although","However","Despite","despite","On the other hand","Even though","even though","In spite of"] → ["although","though","however","despite","in spite of","on the one hand","on the other hand"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 từ nối although / however / despite / on the other hand" → "Có ít nhất 1 từ nối although / however / despite / on the one hand … on the other hand"
- **Bài viết mẫu:**
  - Trước: Ca tru is a traditional kind of singing from northern Vietnam. Although it is very old, few young people know about it. However, some clubs in Hanoi now hold free shows every week. Despite the small audiences, the singers keep practising. On the other hand, some people think it should change to attract young listeners.
  - Sau: Ca tru is a traditional kind of singing from northern Vietnam. Although it is very old, few young people know about it. However, some clubs in Hanoi now hold regular shows. Despite the small audiences, the singers keep practising. On the one hand, it should stay traditional. On the other hand, it needs young listeners.

  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):

    - both (of) + số nhiều | Both of them are real masters. / We can't buy both. | Động từ số nhiều; both có thể đứng một mình
    - either / neither (of) | Do you like either of these? / Neither of them is cheap. | either: câu hỏi, câu phủ định; neither đã mang nghĩa phủ định

- **BT3 (fix)** [Both of them are real masters.] : nhận thêm ["Both of them were real masters."]

### b2-07-8
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Từ nối | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - although / even though + mệnh đề | Although I loved the film, I was nervous. | Even though they were filmed in Phu Yen, …
    - despite / in spite of + danh từ | despite a few changes / in spite of those changes | Không theo sau bởi chủ ngữ + động từ.

    - despite / in spite of + danh từ | In spite of those changes, the feeling was the same. | Không theo sau bởi chủ ngữ + động từ

### b2-08-1
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - have + tân ngữ + V3 | I had my blazer fitted. / I need to have them shortened. | I didn't have it cleaned.
    - get + tân ngữ + V3 | I'll get the trousers shortened. | Thân mật hơn have, nghĩa như nhau.
    - have chia theo thì | had (quá khứ) / am going to have (tương lai) / have had (hoàn thành) | Phân từ (V3) không đổi.
  - Sau:
    - have + tân ngữ + V3 | I had my blazer fitted. | I didn't have it cleaned.
    - get + tân ngữ + V3 (thân mật hơn) | I'll get the trousers shortened. | I won't get the jacket shortened.
    - have chia theo thì, V3 giữ nguyên | I'm going to have my hair cut. / I've had it cleaned. | I haven't had the jacket cleaned yet.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["have","had","having","get","got","getting"] → ["had my","have my","having my","get my","got my","getting my","have it","had it"]

### b2-08-2
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Trường hợp | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - hiện tại tiếp diễn | I'm having a red ao dai made. | I'm not having it made in white.
    - hiện tại hoàn thành | Have you had your nails done? | I haven't had them done yet.
    - + by + người làm | I'm going to have my hair done by a professional. | Chỉ thêm by khi người làm là thông tin quan trọng.
  - Sau:
    - hiện tại tiếp diễn | I'm having a red ao dai made. | I'm not having it made in white.
    - hiện tại hoàn thành | Have you had your nails done? | I haven't had them done yet.
    - + by + người làm (khi cần nói) | I'm going to have my hair done by a professional. | I'm not having my makeup done by a friend.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["have","had","having","get","got","getting"] → ["had her","have her","got her","get her","had my","have my","get my","got my"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu với have / get + tân ngữ + V3" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu nhờ làm: have / get + tân ngữ + V3 (vd. I had my hair cut)"

### b2-08-3
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Trường hợp | Ví dụ | Công thức
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - thói quen | I have my hair styled every second Saturday. | I don't have my shirts ironed.
    - câu hỏi | Do you have your shirts ironed too? | Do / Does + chủ ngữ + have + tân ngữ + V3?
    - khuyên / rủ | I should get mine cleaned. / Let's get your hair cut. | should / let's + get / have + tân ngữ + V3
  - Sau:
    - thói quen | I have my hair styled every second Saturday. / I don't have my shirts ironed. | have / get + tân ngữ + V3
    - câu hỏi | Do you have your shirts ironed too? | Do / Does + chủ ngữ + have + tân ngữ + V3?
    - khuyên / rủ | I should get mine cleaned. / Let's get your hair cut. | should / let's + get / have + tân ngữ + V3
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["have","has","had","get","gets","got"] → ["have my","has his","has her","get my","gets his","gets her","had my","got my"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu với have / get + tân ngữ + V3" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu nhờ làm: have / get + tân ngữ + V3 (vd. I had my hair cut)"

### b2-08-4
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Trường hợp | Ví dụ | Công thức
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - mong muốn | I want to have it dyed. / Would you like to have the buttons changed? | I don't want to have it cut.
    - đề nghị, hỏi | Could we get this jacket dyed? / Can I have flowers stitched? | Could / Can + chủ ngữ + get / have + tân ngữ + V3?
    - hỏi muốn làm gì | What would you like to have done? | done là V3 của do.
  - Sau:
    - mong muốn | I want to have it dyed. / I don't want to have it cut. / Would you like to have the buttons changed? | want to / would like to + have + tân ngữ + V3
    - đề nghị, hỏi | Could we get this jacket dyed? / Can I have flowers stitched? | Could / Can + chủ ngữ + get / have + tân ngữ + V3?
    - hỏi muốn làm gì | What would you like to have done? | What … to have done? (done là V3 của do)
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["have","had","having","get","got","getting"] → ["have it","have them","have my","have the","get it","get them","get my","get the"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu với have / get + tân ngữ + V3" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu nhờ làm: have / get + tân ngữ + V3 (vd. I had my hair cut)"

### b2-08-5
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Trường hợp | Khẳng định | Phủ định
