  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - khuyên (mệnh lệnh) | Get them dry-cleaned. / Have your photos printed. | Don't get them cleaned there.
    - khuyên (can / should) | You can get the zip repaired. | You shouldn't have it repaired there.
    - kể lại (quá khứ) | I had my coat cleaned before my trip. | I didn't have it cleaned.
  - Sau:
    - khuyên (mệnh lệnh) | Get them dry-cleaned. / Have your photos printed. | Don't get them cleaned there.
    - khuyên (can / should) | You can get the zip repaired. | You shouldn't have it repaired there.
    - kể lại (quá khứ) | I had my coat cleaned before my trip. | I didn't have it cleaned.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["have","had","get","got"] → ["get your","have your","had my","got my","get my","have my","get it","have it"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu với have / get + tân ngữ + V3" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu nhờ làm: have / get + tân ngữ + V3 (vd. I had my hair cut)"

### b2-08-6
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Trường hợp | Ví dụ | Công thức
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - mục đích | He has come to have a suit made. | to + have / get + tân ngữ + V3
    - xin yêu cầu | Can I have my name sewn inside? | Can / Could I have … V3?
    - hai V3 nối bằng and | I need to get my shoes cleaned and polished. | Không lặp lại get.
  - Sau:
    - mục đích | He has come to have a suit made. | to + have / get + tân ngữ + V3
    - xin yêu cầu | Can I have my name sewn inside? | Can / Could I have + tân ngữ + V3?
    - hai V3 nối bằng and | I need to get my shoes cleaned and polished. | get + tân ngữ + V3 and V3 (không lặp lại get)
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["have","had","having","get","got"] → ["have it","had it","have my","had my","get my","got my","have them","had the"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu với have / get + tân ngữ + V3" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu nhờ làm: have / get + tân ngữ + V3 (vd. I had my hair cut)"

### b2-08-7
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Trường hợp | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - lời khuyên | Have it altered. / Get it trimmed. | Don't get it cut too short.
    - kế hoạch | I'll get my hair trimmed. / I'll have five copies printed. | I won't have it changed.
    - hạn hoàn thành | She can have it done by Wednesday. | by + thời điểm = trước, chậm nhất là
  - Sau:
    - lời khuyên | Have it altered. / Get it trimmed. | Don't get it cut too short.
    - kế hoạch | I'll get my hair trimmed. / I'll have five copies printed. | I won't have it changed.
    - hạn: by + thời điểm (chậm nhất) | She can have it done by Wednesday. | She can't have it done by Tuesday.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["have","Have","had","get","Get","got"] → ["get your","have your","get it","have it","get them","have them","had my","got my"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu với have / get + tân ngữ + V3" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu nhờ làm: have / get + tân ngữ + V3 (vd. I had my hair cut)"

### b2-08-8
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Nghĩa
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - đã đặt, đang làm | I'm having an evening dress designed. | I'm not having it made in red.
    - tương lai | I'll have the final changes made. | I won't have them painted red.
    - nhờ ai sắp xếp | Can you have my old dress cleaned? | Người được hỏi sẽ mang đồ đi cho người khác làm.
  - Sau:
    - be having + tân ngữ + V3 | I'm having an evening dress designed. / I'm not having it made in red. | Việc đã đặt, đang được làm
    - will have + tân ngữ + V3 | I'll have the final changes made. / I won't have them painted red. | Kế hoạch sẽ nhờ làm
    - Can you have + tân ngữ + V3? | Can you have my old dress cleaned? | Nhờ người kia mang đồ đi cho thợ làm
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["have","had","having","get","got","getting"] → ["having a","having my","having the","have my","have it","had my","get my","got my"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu với have / get + tân ngữ + V3" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu nhờ làm: have / get + tân ngữ + V3 (vd. I had my hair cut)"

## C1

### c1-01-1
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | = câu với if
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Should + S + V | Should sea levels rise, … (= If sea levels rise) | Should the plan not work, … 
    - Were + S + to V / Were + S | Were the government to fund…, … would… (= If the government funded) | Were it not for the charities, … 
    - Had + S + V3 | Had the provinces protected…, … would have / would be… | Had they not acted, … 
    - But for + danh từ | But for the work of a few charities, … would have… | (không có dạng phủ định; bản thân nó đã nghĩa là "nếu không có")
  - Sau:
    - Should + S + V | Should sea levels rise, whole villages could be lost. | If sea levels rise, …
    - Were + S + to V | Were the government to fund the programmes, people would stay. | If the government funded the programmes, …
    - Had + S + V3 | Had the provinces protected the land, farmers would be better off now. | If the provinces had protected the land, …
    - But for + danh từ | But for the work of a few charities, the villages would have disappeared. | If it hadn't been for a few charities, …
    - Were it not for + danh từ | Were it not for the charities, many families would have left. | If it weren't for the charities, …
- **BT1 (fill)** : đề "Điền một từ vào chỗ trống." → "Điền một từ để tạo câu điều kiện đảo ngữ."
- **BT3 (fix)** [Were the government to fund training, people might stay.] : nhận thêm ["Should the government fund training, people might stay."]
- **BT4 (reorder)** [But for the charities, the village would have disappeared.] : nhận thêm ["The village would have disappeared but for the charities."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Were ","Had ","Should ","But for"] → ["but for","had it not","were it not","had we","had they","had i","were we to","should you"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu điều kiện đảo ngữ (Were… to / Had… V3 / Should… / But for…)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu điều kiện đảo ngữ (But for… / Had we/they… + V3 / Had it not been… / Were it not for… / Were we to… / Should you…)"
- **Bài viết mẫu:**
  - Trước: Rising sea levels are a serious threat to the Mekong Delta. Salt water is ruining rice fields, and many young people have already moved to the cities. Had the government protected the mangrove forests earlier, the coast would be much safer today. Furthermore, should the sea rise another half a metre, millions of people could lose their homes. On the whole, I think we need a solution that supports people both in the delta and in the cities.
  - Sau: Rising sea levels are a serious threat to the Mekong Delta. Salt water is ruining rice fields, and many young people have already moved to the cities. Had we protected the mangrove forests earlier, the coast would be much safer today. Furthermore, should the sea rise another half a metre, millions of people could lose their homes. On the whole, I think we need a solution that supports people both in the delta and in the cities.

### c1-01-2
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Chức năng | Từ nối | Ví dụ
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Thêm ý | Furthermore, … / Moreover, … | Moreover, we need honest data.
    - Đối lập | Nevertheless, … / However, … | Nevertheless, I'd question how much…
    - Nhượng bộ | Admittedly, … (rồi However/Nevertheless …) | Admittedly, growth did most of the heavy lifting.
    - Kết quả / tổng kết | Consequently, … / On the whole, … | On the whole, the figures look impressive.
  - Sau:
    - Thêm ý | Furthermore, … / Moreover, … | Moreover, we need honest data.
    - Đối lập | Nevertheless, … / However, … | Nevertheless, I'd question how much…
    - Nhượng bộ | Admittedly, … (rồi However/Nevertheless …) | Admittedly, growth did most of the heavy lifting.
    - Kết quả / tổng kết | Consequently, … / On the whole, … | On the whole, the figures look impressive.
- **BT1 (fill)** : nhận thêm ["Still","Yet"]
- **Dịch T1** [Nevertheless, efficiency remains my biggest concern.]: nhận thêm ["Nevertheless, my biggest concern remains efficiency.","Efficiency nevertheless remains my biggest concern.","My biggest concern nevertheless remains efficiency."]
- **Dịch T4** [Had the clinics stayed open, health care would have improved.]: vi "Nếu các trạm y tế vẫn mở cửa thì dịch vụ y tế đã được cải thiện." → "Nếu khi đó các trạm y tế vẫn tiếp tục mở cửa thì dịch vụ y tế đã được cải thiện."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Furthermore","Moreover","Nevertheless","Admittedly","Consequently","On the whole"] → ["furthermore","moreover","nevertheless","nonetheless","admittedly","consequently","on the whole","in short"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 từ nối diễn ngôn (Furthermore, Moreover, Nevertheless, Admittedly, Consequently, On the whole)" → "Có ít nhất 1 từ nối diễn ngôn (Furthermore, Moreover, Nevertheless, Nonetheless, Admittedly, Consequently, On the whole, In short)"

### c1-01-3
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | = câu với if
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Should + S + V | Should major buyers move production, prices would rise. | Should the talks not succeed, …
    - Were + S + to V | Were tensions to escalate, routes could close. | Were they not to cooperate, …
    - Had + S + V3 | Had car makers kept a second source, they wouldn't have stopped. | Had they not signed the deal, …
    - But for + danh từ | But for trade agreements, firms would have lost buyers. | (tự nó đã mang nghĩa "nếu không có")
  - Sau:
    - Should + S + V | Should the talks not succeed, prices would rise. | If the talks don't succeed, …
    - Were + S + to V | Were tensions to escalate, routes could close. | If tensions escalated, …
    - Had + S + V3 | Had car makers kept a second source, they wouldn't have stopped. | If car makers had kept a second source, …
    - But for + danh từ | But for trade agreements, firms would have lost buyers. | If it hadn't been for trade agreements, …
- **BT4 (reorder)** [But for the agreements, small firms would have failed.] : nhận thêm ["Small firms would have failed but for the agreements."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Were ","Should ","Had ","But for"] → ["but for","had it not","were it not","had we","had they","had i","were we to","should you"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu điều kiện đảo ngữ (Were… to / Should… / Had… V3 / But for…)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu điều kiện đảo ngữ (But for… / Had we/they… + V3 / Had it not been… / Were it not for… / Were we to… / Should you…)"
- **Bài viết mẫu:**
  - Trước: Vietnam depends heavily on exports, so a blocked trade route would be a serious crisis. Should a major port close for a week, factories would quickly run out of parts. Were the closure to last several months, unemployment would rise sharply. Had more companies found a second supplier after 2021, they would be safer today. On the whole, diversification and regional cooperation are the best solutions.
  - Sau: Vietnam depends heavily on exports, so a blocked trade route would be a serious crisis. Should a major port close for a week, factories would quickly run out of parts. Were the closure to last several months, unemployment would rise sharply. But for the second suppliers that many companies found after 2021, the damage would be even worse. On the whole, diversification and regional cooperation are the best solutions.

### c1-01-4
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | = câu với if
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Were + S + to be + V3 | Were the ban to be enforced strictly, waste would drop. | Were the ban not to be enforced, …
    - Had + S + V3 | Had the city acted earlier, the river would be cleaner. | Had the volunteers not removed the rubbish, …
    - Should + S + V | Should the conference ask us, we will recommend a ban. | Should the ban not work, …
    - But for + danh từ | But for public support, even the best law would fail. | (tự nó đã mang nghĩa "nếu không có")
  - Sau:
    - Were + S + to be + V3 | Were the ban to be enforced strictly, waste would drop. | If the ban were enforced strictly, …
    - Had + S + (not) + V3 | Had the volunteers not removed the rubbish, the beaches would have closed. | If the volunteers hadn't removed the rubbish, …
    - Should + S + V | Should the conference ask us, we will recommend a ban. | If the conference asks us, …
    - But for + danh từ | But for public support, even the best law would fail. | If it weren't for public support, …
- **BT3 (fix)** [But for the volunteers, the beaches would have closed.] : nhận thêm ["But for the volunteers' help, the beaches would have closed."]
- **BT4 (reorder)** [But for public support, the law would fail.] : nhận thêm ["The law would fail but for public support."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Were ","Had ","Should ","But for"] → ["but for","had it not","were it not","had we","had they","had i","were we to","should you"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu điều kiện đảo ngữ (Were… to / Had… / Should… / But for…)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu điều kiện đảo ngữ (But for… / Had we/they… + V3 / Had it not been… / Were it not for… / Were we to… / Should you…)"
- **Bài viết mẫu:**
  - Trước: Plastic waste is a serious problem on the beaches near my town. Were the town to ban plastic bags, the amount of rubbish would fall quickly. However, shops would need cheap alternatives. Should the town introduce a deposit scheme instead, people would return bottles, but it would be expensive to run. On the whole, a ban combined with an awareness campaign seems the best solution.
  - Sau: Plastic waste is a serious problem on the beaches near my town. Were we to ban plastic bags, the amount of rubbish would fall quickly. However, shops would need cheap alternatives. Should the town introduce a deposit scheme instead, people would return bottles, but it would be expensive to run. On the whole, a ban combined with an awareness campaign seems the best solution.

### c1-01-5
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Chức năng | Từ nối | Ví dụ
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Đánh giá chung | On the whole, … | On the whole, the region performed better than Europe.
    - Đối lập | Nevertheless, … / However, … | However, early action requires trust.
    - Thêm ý | Furthermore, … / Moreover, … | Moreover, health care workers need protection.
    - Tóm lại | In short, … | In short, preparedness is a matter of trust.
  - Sau:
    - Đánh giá chung | On the whole, … | On the whole, the region performed better than Europe.
    - Đối lập | Nevertheless, … / However, … | However, early action requires trust.
    - Thêm ý | Furthermore, … / Moreover, … | Moreover, health care workers need protection.
    - Tóm lại | In short, … | In short, preparedness is a matter of trust.
- **BT1 (fill)** : nhận thêm ["Still","Yet"]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["On the whole","Nevertheless","Furthermore","Moreover","In short","However"] → ["furthermore","moreover","nevertheless","nonetheless","admittedly","consequently","on the whole","in short"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 từ nối diễn ngôn (On the whole, Nevertheless, Furthermore, Moreover, In short)" → "Có ít nhất 1 từ nối diễn ngôn (Furthermore, Moreover, Nevertheless, Nonetheless, Admittedly, Consequently, On the whole, In short)"

### c1-01-6
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | = câu với if
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Had + S + (not) + V3 | Had experts not helped us, the site would still be in ruins. | Had they not stored old wood, …
    - But for + danh từ | But for their support, the restoration would have taken longer. | (tự nó đã mang nghĩa "nếu không có")
    - Should + S + V | Should a storm hit the city, the pavilions could collapse. | Should the funds not arrive, …
    - Were + S + to be + V3 | Were visitor numbers to be limited, income would fall. | Were the rules not to be followed, …
  - Sau:
    - Had + S + (not) + V3 | Had experts not helped us, the site would still be in ruins. | If experts hadn't helped us, …
    - But for + danh từ | But for their support, the restoration would have taken longer. | If it hadn't been for their support, …
    - Should + S + V | Should a storm hit the city, the pavilions could collapse. | If a storm hits the city, …
    - Were + S + to be + V3 | Were visitor numbers to be limited, income would fall. | If visitor numbers were limited, …
- **BT3 (fix)** [Were the funds to be managed from Paris, craftsmen would be ignored.] : nhận thêm ["Were the funds managed from Paris, craftsmen would be ignored."]
- **BT4 (reorder)** [But for their support, the work would have failed.] : nhận thêm ["The work would have failed but for their support."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Had ","But for","Should ","Were "] → ["but for","had it not","were it not","had we","had they","had i","were we to","should you"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu điều kiện đảo ngữ (Had… / But for… / Should… / Were… to)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu điều kiện đảo ngữ (But for… / Had we/they… + V3 / Had it not been… / Were it not for… / Were we to… / Should you…)"

### c1-01-7
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Chức năng | Từ nối | Ví dụ
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Đánh giá chung | On the whole, … | On the whole, the transition is cheaper than people think.
    - Phản biện | In theory, … Nevertheless, … | Nevertheless, the grid is the problem.
    - Thêm ý | Furthermore, … / Moreover, … | Moreover, they don't need to be imported.
    - Tóm lại | In short, … | In short, renewables are both a climate policy and a security policy.
  - Sau:
    - Đánh giá chung | On the whole, … | On the whole, the transition is cheaper than people think.
    - Phản biện | In theory, … Nevertheless, … | Nevertheless, the grid is the problem.
    - Thêm ý | Furthermore, … / Moreover, … | Moreover, they don't need to be imported.
    - Tóm lại | In short, … | In short, renewables are both a climate policy and a security policy.
- **BT1 (fill)** : nhận thêm ["Additionally","Also"]
- **Dịch T1** [Nevertheless, the grid is the real problem.]: nhận thêm ["The grid is nevertheless the real problem."]
- **Dịch T2** [Should the mines close too quickly, unemployment would rise sharply.]: thẻ nhiễu ["closes","will","jobs"] → ["closes","rose","jobs"]
- **Dịch T4** [Moreover, renewable energy does not need to be imported.]: thẻ nhiễu ["renewal","importing","must"] → ["renewal","exported","must"]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["On the whole","Nevertheless","Furthermore","Moreover","In short"] → ["furthermore","moreover","nevertheless","nonetheless","admittedly","consequently","on the whole","in short"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 từ nối diễn ngôn (On the whole, Nevertheless, Furthermore, Moreover, In short)" → "Có ít nhất 1 từ nối diễn ngôn (Furthermore, Moreover, Nevertheless, Nonetheless, Admittedly, Consequently, On the whole, In short)"

### c1-01-8
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | = câu với if
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Had + S + (not) + V3 | Had its staff not kept paper copies, the consequences would have been worse. | Had they not acted quickly, …
    - Were + S + to V | Were hackers to shut down the grid, hospitals would fail. | Were banks not to delay transfers, …
    - But for + danh từ | But for engineers, millions would have lost power. | (tự nó đã mang nghĩa "nếu không có")
    - Should + S + V | Should a pensioner lose her savings, she may never recover them. | Should the system not respond, …
  - Sau:
    - Had + S + (not) + V3 | Had its staff not kept paper copies, the consequences would have been worse. | If its staff hadn't kept paper copies, …
    - Were + S + to V | Were hackers to shut down the grid, hospitals would fail. | If hackers shut down the grid, …
    - But for + danh từ | But for engineers, millions would have lost power. | If it hadn't been for engineers, …
    - Should + S + V | Should a pensioner lose her savings, she may never recover them. | If a pensioner loses her savings, …
- **BT4 (reorder)** [Were hackers to attack the grid, hospitals would fail.] : nhận thêm ["Hospitals would fail were hackers to attack the grid."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Had ","Were ","But for","Should "] → ["but for","had it not","were it not","had we","had they","had i","were we to","should you"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu điều kiện đảo ngữ (Had… / Were… to / But for… / Should…)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu điều kiện đảo ngữ (But for… / Had we/they… + V3 / Had it not been… / Were it not for… / Were we to… / Should you…)"

### c1-02-1
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Hardly / No sooner | Hardly had + S + V3 … when … / No sooner had + S + V3 … than … | = vừa … thì …
    - Not only / Rarely / Only after | Not only did + S + V …, but S also … / Rarely do + S + V / Only after + V-ing/N + did + S + V | Trợ động từ đứng trước chủ ngữ, động từ chính ở nguyên mẫu
    - do / does / did nhấn mạnh | S + do/does/did + V nguyên mẫu: I do see why it worked. | Không chia động từ chính: did love, không phải did loved
  - Sau:
    - Hardly / No sooner had + S + V3 | Hardly had the product launched when the complaints started. | = vừa … thì …; Hardly đi với when, No sooner đi với than
    - Not only / Rarely + trợ động từ + S | Not only did they cut prices, but they also extended the warranty. | Trợ động từ đứng trước chủ ngữ, động từ chính ở nguyên mẫu
    - Only after + V-ing / N | Only after the reviews appeared did the company react. | Đảo ở mệnh đề chính, không đảo ngay sau Only after
    - do / does / did nhấn mạnh | I do see why it worked. / Plenty of them did love the products. | Không chia động từ chính: did love, không phải did loved
- **BT1 (fill)** : nhận thêm ["will","would","can"]
- **BT3 (fix)** [Only after the meeting did I understand the problem.] : đề "Câu sau có một lỗi. Hãy viết lại cho đúng." → "Câu sau có một lỗi. Hãy viết lại cho đúng, giữ "Only after" ở đầu câu."
- **BT4 (reorder)** [The customers do like the new prices.] : đề "Sắp xếp các từ thành câu đúng." → "Sắp xếp các từ thành câu đúng, có do nhấn mạnh."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["not only","only after","only when","rarely","hardly","no sooner"," do "," did "," does "] → ["not only did","not only does","not only is","only after","only when","rarely do","no sooner had","hardly had"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu đảo ngữ (Not only / Only after / Rarely / Hardly) và 1 câu với do/did nhấn mạnh" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu đảo ngữ (Not only did/does/is… / Only after… / Only when… / Rarely do… / No sooner had… / Hardly had…)"

### c1-02-2
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - do / does / did + V | The cream does work. / She did say that she liked it. | Động từ chính ở nguyên mẫu: does work, không phải does works
    - whatever / whoever | Whatever we decide, … / Whoever wrote this line … | whatever = dù cái gì; whoever = dù ai, bất kỳ ai
    - however + tính từ / trạng từ | however hot the weather gets | Không viết "however the weather gets hot"
  - Sau:
    - do / does / did + V | The cream does work. / She did say that she liked it. | Động từ chính nguyên mẫu: does work, không phải does works
    - whatever / whoever | Whatever we decide, the formula stays. / Whoever wrote this line knew our buyers. | whatever = dù cái gì; whoever = dù ai, bất kỳ ai
    - however + tính từ / trạng từ | However hot the weather gets, the cream keeps skin soft. | Không viết "however the weather gets hot"
- **BT3 (fix)** [However hot the weather is, the cream keeps skin soft.] : đề "Câu sau có một lỗi. Hãy viết lại cho đúng." → "Câu sau có một lỗi. Hãy viết lại cho đúng, vẫn dùng "However"."
- **BT4 (reorder)** [She did say she liked the cream.] : đề "Sắp xếp các từ thành câu đúng." → "Sắp xếp các từ thành câu đúng, có did nhấn mạnh."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["whatever","whoever","however","wherever"," do "," does "," did "] → ["whatever","whoever","wherever","whichever","however much","however hard","however good","however long"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu với do/does/did nhấn mạnh và 1 câu với whatever / whoever / however" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu với whatever / whoever / wherever / whichever, hoặc however + tính từ/trạng từ (however much, however hard…)"
- **Bài viết mẫu:**
  - Trước: Dear customers, last month our advert said that our shampoo stops hair loss in two weeks. That was misleading, and we are sorry. Our shampoo does make hair stronger and cleaner, but it cannot stop hair loss. Whoever bought it because of that promise can get a full refund in any of our shops. Whatever we say in future adverts, we will show the test results online.
  - Sau: Dear customers, last month our advert said that our shampoo stops hair loss in two weeks. That was misleading, and we are sorry. Our shampoo does make hair stronger and cleaner, but it cannot stop hair loss. Whoever bought it because of that promise can get a full refund in any of our shops. Whatever we claim in future adverts, we will prove it with test results online.

### c1-02-3
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - whatever / whoever | whatever we show, … / Whoever buys it, … | whatever = dù cái gì; whoever = dù ai
    - however + tính từ | However different the adverts look, … | = dù … đến đâu
    - Not only / Only after / Rarely | Not only does + S + V … / Only after + N + did + S + V / Rarely does + S + V | Trợ động từ trước chủ ngữ, động từ chính nguyên mẫu
  - Sau:
    - whatever / whoever | Whatever we show, the message must stay the same. / Whoever buys it, the promise is the same. | whatever = dù cái gì; whoever = dù ai
    - however + tính từ | However different the adverts look, the brand must feel the same. | = dù … đến đâu
    - Not only / Only after / Rarely | Only after a long discussion did they find a way. / Rarely does one advert suit everyone. | Trợ động từ trước chủ ngữ, động từ chính nguyên mẫu
- **BT3 (fix)** [Not only does one value travel further, but it is also harder to copy.] : đề "Câu sau có một lỗi. Hãy viết lại cho đúng." → "Câu sau có một lỗi. Hãy viết lại cho đúng, giữ "Not only" ở đầu câu."
- **BT4 (reorder)** [Only after the meeting did they understand the problem.] : đề "Sắp xếp các từ thành câu đúng." → "Sắp xếp thành câu đảo ngữ, bắt đầu bằng "Only after"."; thẻ ["Only","after","the","meeting","did","they","understand","the","problem.","understood"] → ["Only","after","the","meeting","did","they","understand","the","problem.","understands"]; giải thích "Only after + danh từ + did + they + understand; "understood" bị thừa." → "Only after + danh từ + did + they + understand; "understands" bị thừa."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["whatever","whoever","however","wherever","not only","only after","only when","rarely","hardly","no sooner"] → ["whatever","whoever","wherever","not only will","not only did","not only does","only after","rarely do"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu với whatever / whoever / however và 1 câu đảo ngữ" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu với whatever / whoever / wherever, hoặc 1 câu đảo ngữ (Not only will/did/does… / Only after… / Rarely do…)"

### c1-02-4
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Hardly … when | Hardly had + S + V3 … when + quá khứ đơn | = vừa … thì …
    - Only when / Only after | Only when + S + V, did + S + V nguyên mẫu | Chỉ đảo ở mệnh đề chính
    - whatever / whoever / however | whatever it claimed / Whoever copies … / however far they lived | however + tính từ / trạng từ + S + V
  - Sau:
    - Hardly … when | Hardly had the advert gone online when the comments started. | Hardly had + S + V3 … when + quá khứ đơn; = vừa … thì …
    - Only when / Only after | Only when the tests were repeated did the truth come out. | Chỉ đảo ở mệnh đề chính
    - whatever / whoever / however | Whatever it claimed, the cream was ordinary. / Customers came back, however far they lived. | however + tính từ / trạng từ + S + V
- **BT1 (fill)** : nhận thêm ["before"]
- **BT3 (fix)** [Not only did that honesty calm the critics, but it also won customers.] : đề "Câu sau có một lỗi. Hãy viết lại cho đúng." → "Câu sau có một lỗi. Hãy viết lại cho đúng, giữ "Not only" ở đầu câu."
- **BT4 (reorder)** [Hardly had we arrived when it started to rain.] : đề "Sắp xếp các từ thành câu đúng." → "Sắp xếp thành câu đảo ngữ, bắt đầu bằng "Hardly"."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["not only","only when","only after","hardly","no sooner","rarely","whatever","whoever","however"] → ["whatever","whoever","wherever","not only will","not only did","only when","hardly had","rarely do"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu đảo ngữ và 1 câu với whatever / whoever / however" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu với whatever / whoever / wherever, hoặc 1 câu đảo ngữ (Not only will/did… / Only when… / Hardly had… / Rarely do…)"

### c1-02-5
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - do / does / did + V | The cheaper deal does produce real growth. | Động từ chính nguyên mẫu: did bring, không phải did brought
    - Hardly … when | Hardly had + S + V3 … when … | = vừa … thì …
    - Only when / Not only / Rarely | Only when + S + V, do + S + V / Rarely does + S + V | Trợ động từ trước chủ ngữ
  - Sau:
    - do / does / did + V | The cheaper deal does produce real growth. | Động từ chính nguyên mẫu: did bring, không phải did brought
    - Hardly … when | Hardly had his post gone up when sales doubled. | Hardly had + S + V3 … when …; = vừa … thì …
    - Only when / Not only / Rarely | Only when a star uses the product do customers believe it. / Rarely does an advert work alone. | Trợ động từ trước chủ ngữ ở mệnh đề chính
- **BT1 (fill)** : đề "Điền một từ vào chỗ trống." → "Điền một từ để nhấn mạnh (câu khẳng định)."
- **BT3 (fix)** [Hardly had the singer stopped posting when the buyers disappeared.] : đề "Câu sau có một lỗi. Hãy viết lại cho đúng." → "Câu sau có một lỗi. Hãy viết lại cho đúng, giữ "Hardly" ở đầu câu."
- **BT4 (reorder)** [Rarely does transparency cost us anything.] : đề "Sắp xếp các từ thành câu đúng." → "Sắp xếp thành câu đảo ngữ, bắt đầu bằng "Rarely"."
- **Dịch T3** [She has never tried to deceive her fans.]: nhận thêm ["She never tried to deceive her fans."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** [" do "," does "," did ","not only","only when","only after","hardly","rarely","no sooner"] → ["not only did","not only does","not only is","only after","only when","rarely do","no sooner had","hardly had"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu với do/does/did nhấn mạnh và 1 câu đảo ngữ" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu đảo ngữ (Not only did/does/is… / Only after… / Only when… / Rarely do… / No sooner had… / Hardly had…)"

### c1-02-6
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - however + tính từ | however attractive an offer looks | = dù … đến đâu
    - whatever / whoever | whatever she searched for / Whoever designed these apps | whatever = bất kể cái gì; whoever = bất kể ai
    - do / does / did + V | These algorithms do know us. / They did deceive me. | Động từ chính nguyên mẫu: did deceive, không phải did deceived
  - Sau:
    - however + tính từ | However attractive an offer looks, she never buys it. | = dù … đến đâu
    - whatever / whoever | Whatever she searched for, the same ads appeared. / Whoever designed these apps knew us well. | whatever = bất kể cái gì; whoever = bất kể ai
    - do / does / did + V | These algorithms do know us. / They did deceive me. | Động từ chính nguyên mẫu: did deceive, không phải did deceived
- **BT3 (fix)** [However cheap the offer is, I will wait two days.] : đề "Câu sau có một lỗi. Hãy viết lại cho đúng." → "Câu sau có một lỗi. Hãy viết lại cho đúng, vẫn dùng "However"."
- **BT4 (reorder)** [Those algorithms do know us very well.] : đề "Sắp xếp các từ thành câu đúng." → "Sắp xếp các từ thành câu đúng, có do nhấn mạnh."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["however","whatever","whoever","wherever"," do "," does "," did "] → ["whatever","whoever","wherever","however cheap","however much","however good","however hard","however small"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu với however / whatever / whoever và 1 câu với do/does/did nhấn mạnh" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu với whatever / whoever / wherever, hoặc however + tính từ (however cheap, however much…)"

### c1-02-7
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - No sooner … than | No sooner had + S + V3 … than + quá khứ đơn | = vừa … thì …; không dùng when
    - Not only / Only if | Not only was + S …, but S also … / Only if + mệnh đề, would + S + V | Đảo ở mệnh đề chính
    - do / does / did + V | We do know of two patients. / The capsules do help some people. | Động từ chính nguyên mẫu
  - Sau:
    - No sooner … than | No sooner had the advert gone on air than doctors complained. | No sooner had + S + V3 … than; không dùng when
    - Not only | Not only was the claim false, but it was also dangerous. | Đảo ở mệnh đề chính: was the claim
    - Only if | Only if new tests proved the claim would the advert be allowed back. | Đảo ở mệnh đề chính, không đảo sau Only if
    - do / does / did + V | We do know of two patients. / The capsules do help some people. | Động từ chính nguyên mẫu
- **BT3 (fix)** [Rarely have I seen a company lose trust so fast.] : đề "Câu sau có một lỗi. Hãy viết lại cho đúng." → "Câu sau có một lỗi. Hãy viết lại cho đúng, giữ "Rarely" ở đầu câu."
- **BT4 (reorder)** [The capsules do help some people sleep better.] : đề "Sắp xếp các từ thành câu đúng." → "Sắp xếp các từ thành câu đúng, có do nhấn mạnh."
- **Dịch T4** [Only transparency can save the company.]: thẻ nhiễu ["transparent","cannot","saves"] → ["transparent","cannot","saving"]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["no sooner","not only","only if","only after","only when","hardly","rarely"," do "," does "," did "] → ["no sooner had","hardly had","not only was","not only did","not only is","only if","only after","only when"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu đảo ngữ và 1 câu với do/does/did nhấn mạnh" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu đảo ngữ (No sooner had… / Hardly had… / Not only was/did/is… / Only if… / Only after… / Only when…)"

### c1-02-8
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - do / does + V | We do grow it here. / Our coffee does taste fresher. | Nhấn giọng vào do / does khi nói
    - whatever + mệnh đề | Whatever we write, it has to sound like a promise. | = dù … gì
    - whoever + động từ | Whoever drinks it can taste the highlands. | = bất kể ai, ai … cũng
  - Sau:
    - do / does + V | We do grow it here. / Our coffee does taste fresher. | Động từ chính nguyên mẫu; nhấn giọng vào do / does
    - whatever + mệnh đề | Whatever we write, it has to sound like a promise. | = dù … gì
    - whoever + động từ | Whoever drinks it can taste the highlands. | = bất kể ai, ai … cũng
- **BT3 (fix)** [We do grow it here.] : đề "Câu sau có một lỗi. Hãy viết lại cho đúng." → "Câu sau có một lỗi. Hãy viết lại cho đúng, vẫn dùng do/did nhấn mạnh."; nhận thêm ["We did grow it here."]
- **BT4 (reorder)** [Our coffee does taste fresher.] : đề "Sắp xếp các từ thành câu đúng." → "Sắp xếp các từ thành câu đúng, có does nhấn mạnh."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** [" do "," does "," did ","whatever","whoever","however"] → ["whatever","whoever","wherever","whichever","however much","however hard","however good","however long"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu với do/does nhấn mạnh và 1 câu với whatever / whoever / however" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu với whatever / whoever / wherever / whichever, hoặc however + tính từ/trạng từ (however much, however hard…)"

### c1-03-1
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - It is / was … that | It is perfectionism that drives the pressure. | It isn't money that motivates her.
    - It is / was … who (người) | It was my therapist who pointed it out. | It wasn't Duc who wrote the report.
    - What … is / was | What helps most is honest conversation. | What she doesn't need is more pressure.
  - Sau:
    - It is / was … that | It is perfectionism that drives the pressure. | It isn't money that motivates her.
    - It is / was … who (người) | It was my therapist who pointed it out. | It wasn't Duc who wrote the report.
    - What … is / was | What helps most is honest conversation. | What she doesn't need is more pressure.
- **BT3 (fix)** [It is perfectionism that drives most of this pressure.] : nhận thêm ["It is perfectionism which drives most of this pressure."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["it is","it was","what"] → ["it was","it wasn't","it isn't","what helped me was","what i learned was","what i need is","what i remember is"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu chẻ (It is / was … that / who …, What … is …)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu chẻ (It was / wasn't … that / who …, What helped me was …)"

### c1-03-2
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - seem / appear / tend + to V | Shoppers appear to read only good reviews. | They don't seem to trust star ratings.
    - It seems / It would appear / It is likely + that | It is likely that friends matter more. | It is unlikely that ratings matter much.
    - may / might + V | The sample may be too small. | It may not represent older shoppers.
  - Sau:
    - seem / appear / tend + to V | Shoppers appear to read only good reviews. | They don't seem to trust star ratings.
    - It seems / It would appear / It is likely + that | It is likely that friends matter more. | It is unlikely that ratings matter much.
    - may / might + V | The sample may be too small. | It may not represent older shoppers.
- **BT3 (fix)** [Customers who were cheated seem to behave differently.] : nhận thêm ["Customers who were cheated seem to be behaving differently."]
- **BT4 (reorder)** [They appear to read only the positive reviews.] : nhận thêm ["They appear to only read the positive reviews."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["seem","appear","tend","may","might","likely"] → ["seem to","seems to","appear to","appears to","tend to","tends to","is likely","are likely"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 cách diễn đạt rào đón (seem to, appear to, tend to, may, it is likely that)" → "Có ít nhất 1 cách diễn đạt rào đón (seem to, appear to, tend to, is / are likely)"

### c1-03-3
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - It is / was … that | It is trust that rebuilds self-esteem. | It isn't money that exhausts them.
    - What … is / was | What they need is autonomy. | What they don't need is another app.
    - Động từ sau What | What frustrates us is the planning. | Động từ chính (is / was) chia theo phần sau.
  - Sau:
    - It is / was … that | It is trust that rebuilds self-esteem. | It isn't money that exhausts them.
    - What … is / was | What they need is autonomy. | What they don't need is another app.
    - What + mệnh đề + was | What exhausted them was the constant planning. | What they didn't get was any real autonomy.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["it is","it isn't","it was","what"] → ["it isn't","it is not","it's not","it is the","it was the","what we need is","what students need is","what people need is"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu chẻ (It is / isn't … that …, What … is …)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu chẻ (It isn't … that …, It is the … that …, What we need is …)"

### c1-03-4
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - tend / seem / appear + to V | People tend to protect their self-esteem. | He doesn't seem to notice it.
    - may / might + V | There may be other options. | It might not change overnight.
    - It is likely / It would appear + that | It is likely that your instinct is right. | It is unlikely that they will refuse.
  - Sau:
    - tend / seem / appear + to V | People tend to protect their self-esteem. | He doesn't seem to notice it.
    - may / might + V | There may be other options. | It might not change overnight.
    - It is likely / It would appear + that | It is likely that your instinct is right. | It is unlikely that they will refuse.
- **BT3 (fix)** [The whole team seems to be relaxed about the campaign.] : nhận thêm ["The whole team seems relaxed about the campaign."]
- **Dịch T1** [The process appears to be largely unconscious.]: nhận thêm ["The process appears largely unconscious."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["seem","appear","tend","may","might","likely"] → ["tend to","tends to","seem to","seems to","appear to","appears to","is likely","might"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 cách diễn đạt rào đón (tend to, seem to, may, might, it is likely that)" → "Có ít nhất 1 cách diễn đạt rào đón (tend to, seem to, appear to, might, it is likely that)"

### c1-03-5
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - It was … that (vật, sự việc) | It was diffusion of responsibility that stopped them. | It wasn't a lack of empathy that kept them still.
    - It was … who (người) | It was a retired nurse who helped him. | It wasn't the police who arrived first.
    - What … is / was | What surprised me was the silence. | What they didn't do was call for help.
  - Sau:
    - It was … that (vật, sự việc) | It was diffusion of responsibility that stopped them. | It wasn't a lack of empathy that kept them still.
    - It was … who (người) | It was a retired nurse who helped him. | It wasn't the police who arrived first.
    - What … is / was | What surprised me was the silence. | What they didn't do was call for help.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["it was","it wasn't","it is","what"] → ["it was","it wasn't","it was not","what i learned was","what surprised me was","what i remember is","what struck me was"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu chẻ (It was … that / who …, What … was …)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu chẻ (It was / wasn't … that / who …, What I learned was …)"

### c1-03-6
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - seem + tính từ / to V | Audiences seem less critical than we think. | Nobody seemed to notice.
    - tend to + V | Speakers tend to overestimate attention. | Listeners don't tend to remember small errors.
    - probably / may / It would appear that | They will probably feel empathy. | They probably won't notice.
  - Sau:
    - seem + tính từ / to V | Audiences seem less critical than we think. | Nobody seemed to notice.
    - tend to + V | Speakers tend to overestimate attention. | Listeners don't tend to remember small errors.
    - probably / may / It would appear that | They will probably feel empathy. | They probably won't notice.
- **BT1 (fill)** : nhận thêm ["seem","appear"]; giải thích "tend to + V: có xu hướng." → "tend / seem / appear + to V."
- **BT4 (reorder)** [They probably won't notice.] : nhận thêm ["They probably won't notice.","Probably they won't notice."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["seem","appear","tend","probably","may","might","likely"] → ["tend to","tends to","seem to","seems to","probably","it would appear","might","is likely"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 cách diễn đạt rào đón (seem, tend to, probably, may, it would appear that)" → "Có ít nhất 1 cách diễn đạt rào đón (tend to, seem to, probably, might, it would appear that)"

### c1-03-7
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - What + mệnh đề + is | What hurts investors most is losing money. | What they don't see is the long-term trend.
    - It is / isn't … that | It is fear that drives these decisions. | It isn't the numbers that drive them.
    - Nhấn mạnh người | It's Mr Thang that / who I'll call first. | It wasn't Ms Hoa who sold.
  - Sau:
    - What + mệnh đề + is | What hurts investors most is losing money. | What they don't see is the long-term trend.
    - It is / isn't … that | It is fear that drives these decisions. | It isn't the numbers that drive them.
    - Nhấn mạnh người | It's Mr Thang that / who I'll call first. | It wasn't Ms Hoa who sold.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["what","it was","it wasn't","it is"] → ["it was","it wasn't","it was not","what i learned was","what i realised was","what he realised was","what scared me was","what mattered was"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu chẻ (What … was …, It was / wasn't … that …)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu chẻ (It was / wasn't … that …, What I learned / realised was …)"

### c1-03-8
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - It would appear / It is likely + that | It would appear that we ignored the risk. | It is unlikely that one person caused it.
    - seem / tend + to V | Teams tend to follow the first strong opinion. | Nobody seemed to disagree.
    - may / might / could + V | A critic role might prevent mistakes. | People may not speak up at first.
  - Sau:
    - It would appear / It is likely + that | It would appear that we ignored the risk. | It is unlikely that one person caused it.
    - seem / tend + to V | Teams tend to follow the first strong opinion. | Nobody seemed to disagree.
    - may / might / could + V | A critic role might prevent mistakes. | People may not speak up at first.
- **Dịch T1** [The process seems to be largely unconscious.]: nhận thêm ["The process seems largely unconscious."]
- **Dịch T3** [It would appear that we all shared the same bias.]: vi "Có vẻ như tất cả chúng ta đều có cùng một thiên kiến." → "Có vẻ như tất cả chúng ta đã có cùng một thiên kiến."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["seem","appear","tend","may","might","likely"] → ["seem to","seems to","seemed to","tend to","tends to","it would appear","is likely","might"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 cách diễn đạt rào đón (seem to, tend to, may, might, it would appear that, it is likely that)" → "Có ít nhất 1 cách diễn đạt rào đón (seem to, tend to, might, it would appear that, it is likely that)"

### c1-04-1
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - It + is + V3 + that | It is thought / believed / said that + mệnh đề: It is thought that investors lost money. | It is not thought that + mệnh đề
    - Chủ ngữ + is + V3 + to V (cùng lúc) | He is reported to be hiding abroad. / He is said to be talking. | He is not believed to be + …
    - Chủ ngữ + is + V3 + to have V3 (xảy ra trước) | He is believed to have fled. / They are thought to have known. | He is not thought to have + V3
  - Sau:
    - It + is + V3 + that | It is thought that investors lost money. | It is not thought that the bank knew.
    - S + is + V3 + to V (cùng lúc) | He is reported to be hiding abroad. | He is not believed to be working alone.
    - S + is + V3 + to have V3 (xảy ra trước) | He is believed to have fled the country. | They are not thought to have known.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["is believed to","is said to","is thought to","is reported to","are believed to","are said to","are thought to","it is believed that","it is thought that","it is said that"] → ["is said to","are said to","is believed","are believed","is thought","are thought","is reported to","are reported to"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu bị động tường thuật (it is believed that … / is said to have …)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu bị động tường thuật (it is believed / thought that … / is said to have …)"

### c1-04-2
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - insist / demand / ask that | S + insist that + S + V nguyên mẫu: I insist that every guard remain vigilant. | insist that he not leave the entrance
    - recommend / suggest that | S + suggest that + S + V nguyên mẫu: I suggest that one supervisor check the recordings. | suggest that they not use force
    - Bị động | that + S + be + V3: I recommend that the protocols be implemented. | that the photos not be published
  - Sau:
    - insist / demand / ask that + S + V | I insist that every guard remain vigilant. | I insist that he not leave the entrance.
    - recommend / suggest that + S + V | I suggest that one supervisor check the recordings. | I suggest that they not use force.
    - Bị động: that + S + be + V3 | I recommend that the protocols be implemented. | I ask that the photos not be published.
- **BT3 (fix)** [The manager suggested that Tuan check the recordings every day.] : nhận thêm ["The manager suggested that Tuan should check the recordings every day.","The manager suggested Tuan check the recordings every day.","The manager suggested Tuan should check the recordings every day."]
- **Dịch T4** [The shop owner suggested that the man be taken to court.]: thẻ nhiễu ["taking","suggests","prison"] → ["takes","suggests","prison"]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["insist that","insists that","insisted that","recommend that","recommends that","recommended that","suggest that","suggests that","suggested that","demand that"] → ["insist that","insists that","insisted that","recommend that","recommended that","suggest that","suggested that","demand that"]

### c1-04-3
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - It is thought that + mệnh đề | It is thought that the hackers accessed three servers. | It is not thought that any money was taken.
    - S + is believed + to have V3 | They are believed to have copied the data. | They are not believed to have taken any money.
    - suggest / recommend that + S + V nguyên mẫu | I suggest that the board update all credentials. | I suggest that staff not open strange links.
  - Sau:
    - It is thought that + mệnh đề | It is thought that the hackers accessed three servers. | It is not thought that any money was taken.
    - S + is believed + to have V3 | They are believed to have copied the data. | They are not believed to have taken any money.
    - suggest / recommend that + S + V nguyên mẫu | I suggest that the board update all credentials. | I suggest that staff not open strange links.
- **BT1 (fill)** : nhận thêm ["reset"]
- **Dịch T2** [They are believed to steal data from banks.]: vi "Người ta tin rằng chúng chuyên lấy cắp dữ liệu từ các ngân hàng." → "Người ta tin rằng chúng lấy cắp dữ liệu từ các ngân hàng."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["it is thought that","it is believed that","is thought to","is believed to","are believed to","are thought to","suggest that","recommend that","insist that"] → ["is thought","is believed","are thought","are believed","is said to","suggest that","recommend that","insist that"]
- **Bài viết mẫu:**
  - Trước: Dear Ms Lan, last Friday a laptop was taken from the sales office. It is thought that the thief entered the building with a group of visitors. The laptop is believed to contain the contact details of about two hundred customers. I have already reported the theft to the police. I suggest that every visitor be given a badge at reception, and I recommend that all laptops use a password. Best regards, Duy.
  - Sau: Dear Ms Lan, last Friday a laptop was taken from the sales office. It is thought that the thief entered the building with a group of visitors. The laptop is believed to contain the contact details of about two hundred customers. I have already reported the theft to the police. I suggest that every visitor be given a badge at reception, and I recommend that all laptops be protected by a password. Best regards, Duy.

### c1-04-4
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - propose / recommend that + S + be + V3 | I propose that fines be doubled. | We propose that fines not be reduced.
    - S + is / are said + to have V3 | Incidents are said to have decreased by a third. | Fines are not said to have changed anything.
    - It is believed / thought that + mệnh đề | It is believed that cameras change behaviour. | It is not believed that higher fines alone will work.
  - Sau:
    - propose / recommend that + S + be + V3 | I propose that fines be doubled. | We propose that fines not be reduced.
    - S + is / are said + to have V3 | Incidents are said to have decreased by a third. | Fines alone are said not to have changed much.
    - It is believed / thought that + mệnh đề | It is believed that cameras change behaviour. | It is not believed that higher fines alone will work.
- **BT3 (fix)** [The proposal recommends that every school run a road safety course.] : nhận thêm ["The proposal recommends that every school should run a road safety course."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["propose that","recommend that","suggest that","insist that","is said to","are said to","it is believed that","it is thought that","is believed to","are believed to"] → ["propose that","recommend that","suggest that","insist that","is said to","are said to","is believed","is thought"]

### c1-04-5
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - S + is alleged + to have V3 | He is alleged to have laundered money. | He is not alleged to have used violence.
    - S + is said + to be V-ing | One witness is said to be cooperating. | He is not said to be cooperating.
    - It is thought that + mệnh đề | It is thought that the money has left the country. | It is not thought that he acted alone.
  - Sau:
    - S + is alleged + to have V3 | He is alleged to have laundered money. | He is not alleged to have used violence.
    - S + is said + to be V-ing | One witness is said to be cooperating. | The banker is said not to be cooperating.
    - It is thought that + mệnh đề | It is thought that the money has left the country. | It is not thought that he acted alone.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["is alleged to","are alleged to","is said to","are said to","is reported to","are reported to","is estimated to","it is thought that","it is believed that","it is alleged that"] → ["is alleged","are alleged","is said to","are said to","is reported to","is estimated","is thought","is believed"]

### c1-04-6
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Hiện tại: demand / insist that | I demand that the company enforce the policy. | I demand that the policy not be delayed.
    - Quá khứ: insisted / suggested that | She insisted that each manager sign a statement. | She insisted that they not accept gifts.
    - Bị động: that + S + be + V3 | She suggested that audits be conducted annually. | She asked that the payments not be hidden.
  - Sau:
    - Hiện tại: demand / insist that | I demand that the company enforce the policy. | I demand that the policy not be delayed.
    - Quá khứ: insisted / suggested that | She insisted that each manager sign a statement. | She insisted that they not accept gifts.
    - Bị động: that + S + be + V3 | She suggested that audits be conducted annually. | She asked that the payments not be hidden.
- **BT1 (fill)** : nhận thêm ["implement","apply","follow"]
- **BT3 (fix)** [Mr Hoang recommended that the board approve the policy on Friday.] : nhận thêm ["Mr Hoang recommended that the board should approve the policy on Friday."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["demand that","demanded that","insist that","insisted that","suggest that","suggested that","recommend that","recommended that","ask that","asked that"] → ["demand that","demanded that","insist that","insisted that","recommend that","recommended that","suggest that","suggested that"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu thức giả định (demand / insist / recommend that + S + V nguyên mẫu)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu thức giả định (demand / insist / recommend / suggest that + S + V nguyên mẫu)"

### c1-04-7
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - S + is reported + to have V3 | The thieves are reported to have disabled the cameras. | They are not reported to have used weapons.
    - S + is said + to have been … | The guard is said to have been asleep. | He is not said to have been involved.
    - insist that + S + be + V3 | I insist that the premises be searched. | I insist that the scene not be touched.
  - Sau:
    - S + is reported + to have V3 | The thieves are reported to have disabled the cameras. | They are not reported to have used weapons.
    - S + is said + to have been … | The guard is said to have been asleep. | The second guard is said not to have been involved.
    - insist that + S + be + V3 | I insist that the premises be searched. | I insist that the scene not be touched.
- **BT1 (fill)** : nhận thêm ["fallen"]; giải thích "is said + to have been + tính từ." → "is said + to have been + tính từ (been asleep), hoặc to have fallen asleep (đã ngủ thiếp đi)."
- **BT3 (fix)** [Thuy recommended that Mr Cuong give them a list of staff.] : nhận thêm ["Thuy recommended that Mr Cuong should give them a list of staff."]
- **Dịch T2** [I insist that the police officer check every nearby factory.]: nhận thêm ["I insist that the police officer check every factory nearby.","I insist the police officer check every factory nearby."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["is reported to","are reported to","is said to","are said to","is thought to","it is believed that","it is thought that","insist that","recommend that","suggest that"] → ["is reported to","are reported to","is said to","are said to","is thought","are thought","insist that","recommend that"]

### c1-04-8
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - S + is understood + to have V3 | The runoff is understood to have endangered the water. | It is not understood to have reached the city.
    - S + is said + to have been V-ing | Families are said to have been drinking polluted water. | They are not said to have been warned.
    - urge / demand that + S + be + V3 | We urge that the managers be prosecuted. | We demand that the case not be closed.
  - Sau:
    - S + is understood + to have V3 | The runoff is understood to have endangered the water. | The runoff is understood not to have reached the city.
    - S + is said + to have been V-ing | Families are said to have been drinking polluted water. | They are said not to have been warned.
    - urge / demand that + S + be + V3 | We urge that the managers be prosecuted. | We demand that the case not be closed.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["is understood to","is said to","are said to","is believed to","is thought to","it is believed that","it is thought that","urge that","demand that","recommend that"] → ["is understood to","is said to","are said to","is believed","is thought","urge that","demand that","recommend that"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu bị động tường thuật hoặc thức giả định (is understood to have … / urge that … be + V3)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu bị động tường thuật hoặc thức giả định (is believed / understood to have … / urge that … be + V3)"

### c1-05-1
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cách dùng | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Dự định cũ | was / were going to + V: he was going to sell the business | wasn't / weren't going to + V
    - Sắp sửa | was / were about to + V: we were all about to lose our jobs | wasn't / weren't about to + V
    - Về sau (nhìn từ quá khứ) | would + V; was / were to + V: that system was to become… | wouldn't + V
  - Sau:
    - Dự định cũ | was / were going to + V: he was going to sell the business | wasn't / weren't going to + V
    - Sắp sửa | was / were about to + V: we were all about to lose our jobs | wasn't / weren't about to + V
    - Về sau (nhìn từ quá khứ) | would + V; was / were to + V: that system was to become… | wouldn't + V; was never to + V
- **BT1 (fill)** : nhận thêm ["going"]; giải thích "Sắp sửa xảy ra thì bị chen ngang: were about to + V." → "Sắp sửa xảy ra thì bị chen ngang: were about to + V (were going to + V cũng đúng)."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["was going to","were going to","was about to","were about to","would"] → ["was going to","were going to","was about to","were about to","was to","would become","would later","would one day"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu was / were going to, was / were about to hoặc would" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu tương lai trong quá khứ (was / were going to, was / were about to, was to, would later / would become / would one day …)"

### c1-05-2
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cách dùng | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Sắp sửa | was / were about to + V: He was about to relocate | wasn't / weren't about to + V
    - Dự định, lịch đã định | was going to + V; was to + V: his ship was to leave | wasn't going to + V
    - Về sau mới đến | would + V: the sketches would become valuable | wouldn't + V
  - Sau:
    - Sắp sửa | was / were about to + V: He was about to relocate | wasn't / weren't about to + V
    - Dự định, lịch đã định | was going to + V; was to + V: his ship was to leave | wasn't going to + V; wasn't to + V
    - Về sau mới đến | would + V: the sketches would become valuable | wouldn't + V
- **BT1 (fill)** : bỏ ["due to"]; giải thích "Lịch đã định trong quá khứ: was to + V (hoặc was due to + V)." → "Lịch đã định trong quá khứ: was to + V."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["was about to","were about to","was going to","were going to","was to","would"] → ["was about to","were about to","was going to","were going to","was to","would later","would change","would one day"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu was about to, was going to, was to hoặc would" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu tương lai trong quá khứ (was / were going to, was / were about to, was to, would later / would become / would one day …)"

### c1-05-3
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cách dùng | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Dự định cũ | was going to + V: I was going to accept a job | wasn't going to + V
    - Đã sắp xếp | was to + V: I was to start on the first of June | wasn't to + V
    - Sắp sửa / về sau | was about to + V … when …; would + V | wouldn't + V
  - Sau:
    - Dự định cũ | was going to + V: I was going to accept a job | wasn't going to + V
    - Đã sắp xếp | was to + V: I was to start on the first of June | wasn't to + V
    - Sắp sửa / về sau | was about to + V … when …; would + V | wasn't about to + V; wouldn't + V
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["was going to","were going to","was about to","were about to","was to","would"] → ["was going to","were going to","was about to","were about to","was to","would change","would later","would become"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu was going to, was to, was about to hoặc would" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu tương lai trong quá khứ (was / were going to, was / were about to, was to, would later / would become / would one day …)"

### c1-05-4
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cách dùng | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Sắp sửa | was / were about to + V: he was about to give up music | wasn't about to + V
    - Dự định cũ | was / were going to + V: he was going to sell his guitar | wasn't going to + V
    - Về sau mới đến | would + V: the song would become an anthem | wouldn't + V
  - Sau:
    - Sắp sửa | was / were about to + V: he was about to give up music | wasn't about to + V
    - Dự định cũ | was / were going to + V: he was going to sell his guitar | wasn't going to + V
    - Về sau mới đến | would + V: the song would become an anthem | wouldn't + V
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["was about to","were about to","was going to","were going to","would"] → ["was about to","were about to","was going to","were going to","would change","would later","would become","would one day"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu was about to, was going to hoặc would" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu tương lai trong quá khứ (was / were going to, was / were about to, was to, would later / would become / would one day …)"

### c1-05-5
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cách dùng | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Dự định cũ | was going to + V: she was going to publish it anonymously | wasn't going to + V
    - Sắp sửa | was about to + V: She was about to become famous | wasn't about to + V
    - Về sau đã đến | would + V; was to + V: she was to win a major prize | wouldn't + V; was never to + V
  - Sau:
    - Dự định cũ | was going to + V: she was going to publish it anonymously | wasn't going to + V
    - Sắp sửa | was about to + V: She was about to become famous | wasn't about to + V
    - Về sau đã đến | would + V; was to + V: she was to win a major prize | wouldn't + V; was never to + V
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["was going to","were going to","was about to","were about to","was to","would"] → ["was going to","were going to","was about to","were about to","was to","would go on","would later","would become"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu was going to, was about to, would hoặc was to" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu tương lai trong quá khứ (was / were going to, was / were about to, was to, would later / would become / would one day …)"

### c1-05-6
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cách dùng | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Đã được giao, định sẵn | was / were to + V: he was to lead the mission | was never to + V
    - Sắp sửa (bị động) | was / were about to be + V3: the agreement was about to be finalised | wasn't about to be + V3
    - Về sau mới đến | would + V: this failure would shape his life | wouldn't + V
  - Sau:
    - Đã được giao, định sẵn | was / were to + V: he was to lead the mission | wasn't / weren't to + V; was never to + V
    - Sắp sửa (bị động) | was / were about to be + V3: the agreement was about to be finalised | wasn't about to be + V3
    - Về sau mới đến | would + V: this failure would shape his life | wouldn't + V
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["was to","were to","was about to","were about to","was going to","would"] → ["was to","were to","was about to","were about to","was going to","were going to","would later","would become"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu was to, was about to, was going to hoặc would" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu tương lai trong quá khứ (was / were going to, was / were about to, was to, would later / would become / would one day …)"

### c1-05-7
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cách dùng | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Kế hoạch cũ | was going to + V: He was going to design a huge hall | wasn't going to + V
    - Sắp bắt đầu | was about to + V: work was about to start | wasn't about to + V
    - Tương lai nhìn từ quá khứ | told / said + would + V: the station would have to be smaller | would not + V
  - Sau:
    - Kế hoạch cũ | was going to + V: He was going to design a huge hall | wasn't going to + V
    - Sắp bắt đầu | was about to + V: work was about to start | wasn't about to + V
    - Tương lai nhìn từ quá khứ | told / said + would + V: the station would have to be smaller | would not + V
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["was going to","were going to","was about to","were about to","would"] → ["was going to","were going to","was about to","were about to","would have to","would become","would later","would one day"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu was going to, was about to hoặc would" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu tương lai trong quá khứ (was / were going to, was / were about to, was to, would later / would become / would one day …)"

### c1-05-8
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cách dùng | Khẳng định | Phủ định
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Lời dự đoán lúc đó | told / said that + would + V: he would never be a leading man | would never / wouldn't + V
    - Sắp làm | was / were about to + V: he was about to quit acting | wasn't about to + V
    - Dự định cũ | was / were going to + V: he was going to move there | wasn't going to + V
  - Sau:
    - Lời dự đoán lúc đó | told / said that + would + V: he would never be a leading man | would never / wouldn't + V
    - Sắp làm | was / were about to + V: he was about to quit acting | wasn't about to + V
    - Dự định cũ | was / were going to + V: he was going to move there | wasn't going to + V
- **BT1 (fill)** : nhận thêm ["offer","hand","assign"]; giải thích "was about to + động từ nguyên mẫu: give." → "was about to + động từ nguyên mẫu: give (offer / hand / assign cũng đúng)."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["was about to","were about to","was going to","were going to","would"] → ["was about to","were about to","was going to","were going to","would offer","would later","would become","would one day"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu was about to, was going to hoặc would" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu tương lai trong quá khứ (was / were going to, was / were about to, was to, would later / would become / would one day …)"

### c1-06-1
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - think / suppose / be afraid / hope + so / not | I think so. / I'm afraid so. / I hope not. | I don't think so. / I hope not (không nói I don't hope so)
    - one / ones thay danh từ đếm được | the old one / new ones / the simple ones | Không dùng ones cho danh từ không đếm được (information, advice)
    - do so thay cụm động từ | If they can do so by Friday, … | Trang trọng hơn do it / do that
    - to thay động từ nguyên mẫu | I'd love to. / I've tried to. / I'm going to. | I'd love to, but … / I'd rather not.
  - Sau:
    - think / suppose / be afraid / hope + so / not | I think so. / I'm afraid so. / I hope not. | Phủ định: I don't think so / I hope not (không nói I don't hope so)
    - one / ones thay danh từ đếm được | the old one / new ones / the simple ones | Không dùng ones cho danh từ không đếm được (information, advice)
    - do so thay cụm động từ | If they can do so by Friday, … | Trang trọng hơn do it / do that
    - to thay động từ nguyên mẫu | I'd love to. / I've tried to. / I'm going to. | Từ chối nhẹ: I'd love to, but … / I'd rather not.
- **BT4 (reorder)** [If they can do so by Friday, the video can go online.] : nhận thêm ["The video can go online if they can do so by Friday."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["I think so","I hope not","afraid so","afraid not","ones","one","do so","love to","so","not"] → ["think so","hope not","afraid so","afraid not","do so","love to","ones"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu rút gọn / thay thế (so, not, one / ones, do so, to)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu rút gọn / thay thế (I think so, I hope not, I'm afraid so / not, ones, do so, I'd love to)"
- **Bài viết mẫu:**
  - Trước: "Do people in Hue understand this Northern idiom?" Mai asked. "Some do, but I don't think most young people would," Khanh said. "Should we use a different one?" "I think so. Let's choose a simpler one and test it with a few customers." "Good idea. Can you do so before Monday?" "I'll try to. Would you like to help me?" "I'd love to."
  - Sau: "Do people in Hue understand this Northern idiom?" Mai asked. "Some do, but I don't think most young people do," Khanh said. "Should we use a different one?" "I think so. Let's choose a simpler one and test it with a few customers." "Good idea. Can you do so before Monday?" "I'll try to. Would you like to help me?" "I'd love to."

### c1-06-2
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - do so thay cụm động từ | if they can do so by the deadline / Then do so today. | not do so: If you decide not to do so, tell me.
    - say / suppose / think + so | someone had to say so / I suppose so | I don't suppose so / I don't think so
    - to thay động từ nguyên mẫu | If you wish to, … / Would you like to? / Someone did need to. | I'd prefer not to.
  - Sau:
    - do so thay cụm động từ | if they can do so by the deadline / Then do so today. | Phủ định: not do so (If you decide not to do so, tell me.)
    - say / suppose / think + so | someone had to say so / I suppose so | Phủ định: I don't suppose so / I don't think so
    - to thay động từ nguyên mẫu | If you wish to, … / Would you like to? / Someone did need to. | Phủ định: I'd prefer not to.
- **BT2 (choice)** : phương án ["to","it","so","that"] → ["to","it","so","for"]
- **BT4 (reorder)** [If you wish to, we can go through the structure together.] : nhận thêm ["We can go through the structure together if you wish to."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["do so","say so","suppose so","afraid so","wish to","like to","love to","so","to"] → ["do so","say so","think so","suppose so","afraid so","hope not","wish to","love to"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu rút gọn / thay thế (do so, so, to)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu rút gọn / thay thế (do so, say so, I think / suppose / am afraid so, I hope not, if you wish to, I'd love to)"

### c1-06-3
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Bỏ phần lặp, giữ trợ động từ | Forty have signed, twelve haven't yet. | Trợ động từ phải cùng loại: have … haven't, will … won't
    - and again / and also / too | Plastic down in 2024, and again in 2025. | Không lặp lại chủ ngữ và động từ.
    - one / ones | the next one / the old one / that one | Không dùng a one khi không có tính từ: Do you need one?
    - so / not; do so | I think so. / I don't think so. / let's do so now. | I hope not (không nói I don't hope so)
  - Sau:
    - Bỏ phần lặp, giữ trợ động từ | Forty have signed, twelve haven't yet. | Trợ động từ phải cùng loại: have … haven't, will … won't
    - and again / and also / too | Plastic down in 2024, and again in 2025. | Không lặp lại chủ ngữ và động từ.
    - one / ones | the next one / the old one / that one | Không dùng a one khi không có tính từ: Do you need one?
    - so / not; do so | I think so. / I don't think so. / let's do so now. | Nói I hope not, không nói I don't hope so
- **Dịch T2** [It sounds positive but conveys almost nothing.]: thẻ nhiễu ["anything","negative","hardly"] → ["negative","positively","conveying"]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["one","ones","so","not","do so","again","haven't","hasn't","won't"] → ["do so","think so","hope not","afraid not","haven't yet","hasn't yet","and again","ones"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu lược bỏ / thay thế (one / ones, so / not, do so, and again …)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu lược bỏ / thay thế (… haven't yet, and again …, ones, I think so / I hope not, do so)"

### c1-06-4
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - hope / expect / be afraid + so / not | I expect so. / I hope not. / I'm afraid not. | I don't expect so = I expect not (trang trọng hơn)
    - plan / want / promise + to | We plan to. / She promised to. | We don't plan to. / We'd prefer not to.
    - trợ động từ thay cụm động từ | I can't promise it won't. | Trợ động từ theo thì của câu trước: will … won't
  - Sau:
    - hope / expect / be afraid + so / not | I expect so. / I hope not. / I'm afraid not. | Phủ định: I don't expect so = I expect not (trang trọng hơn)
    - plan / want / promise + to | We plan to. / She promised to. | Phủ định: We don't plan to. / We'd prefer not to.
    - trợ động từ thay cụm động từ | I can't promise it won't. | Trợ động từ theo thì của câu trước: will … won't
- **BT1 (fill)** : đề "Điền một từ vào chỗ trống." → "Điền một từ vào chỗ trống. Quang nghĩ là vụ thu hoạch sẽ nhỏ hơn."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["hope not","expect so","afraid not","afraid so","plan to","promised to","do so","so","not"] → ["hope not","hope so","expect so","expect not","afraid not","afraid so","think so","do so"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu rút gọn (I hope not, I expect so, plan to, do so …)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu rút gọn (I hope not, I expect so, I'm afraid so / not, I think so, do so)"

### c1-06-5
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - the former / the latter | the former says 'răng' and the latter says 'sao' | Chỉ dùng khi có đúng hai thứ đã nhắc.
    - one / ones | which one / the first one / the ones in the north | Không dùng cho danh từ không đếm được.
    - do so | if I can do so politely / please do so | if I can't (do so), …
    - trợ động từ | Most people did. / It doesn't to us. / They should. | Trợ động từ theo câu trước: find … did, should learn … should
  - Sau:
    - the former / the latter | the former says 'răng' and the latter says 'sao' | Chỉ dùng khi có đúng hai thứ đã nhắc.
    - one / ones | which one / the first one / the ones in the north | Không dùng cho danh từ không đếm được.
    - do so | if I can do so politely / please do so | Phủ định: if I can't (do so), …
    - trợ động từ | Most people did. / It doesn't to us. / They should. | Trợ động từ theo câu trước: find … did, should learn … should
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["former","latter","one","ones","do so","so","did","does","should"] → ["the former","the latter","do so","think so","hope not","afraid not","ones"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu thay thế khi so sánh (the former / the latter, one, do so, trợ động từ)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu thay thế khi so sánh (the former / the latter, ones, do so, I think so / I hope not)"

### c1-06-6
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Lưu ý
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - If so / If not | Is this acceptable? If so, … If not, … | If not (không dùng If no / If not so)
    - hope / think / be afraid + so / not | I hope so. / I think so. / I'm afraid not. | I don't think so. / I hope not.
    - say so / do so | Then say so. / we'll do so together | Không thêm it: say so (không phải say it so)
    - to cuối câu | if you would like us to / I'd be happy to | I'd prefer not to.
  - Sau:
    - If so / If not | Is this acceptable? If so, … If not, … | Không nói If no / If not so
    - hope / think / be afraid + so / not | I hope so. / I think so. / I'm afraid not. | Phủ định: I don't think so. / I hope not.
    - say so / do so | Then say so. / we'll do so together | Không thêm it: say so (không phải say it so)
    - to cuối câu | if you would like us to / I'd be happy to | Phủ định: I'd prefer not to.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["If so","if so","If not","if not","hope so","afraid not","think so","us to","do so","so","not"] → ["if so","if not","hope so","hope not","think so","afraid not","like us to","do so"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 1 câu dùng If so / If not hoặc so / not thay mệnh đề" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu dùng If so / If not hoặc so / not thay mệnh đề (I hope so, I think so, I'm afraid not, do so)"
- **Bài viết mẫu:**
  - Trước: Dear Ms Hoa, our team meeting will take place on Monday at 10 a.m. Can you attend? If so, no reply is needed. If not, please let us know which day suits you, and we will change the date if we can do so. We can also send you the slides in advance, if you would like us to. Best regards, Tuan
  - Sau: Dear Ms Hoa, our team meeting will take place on Monday at 10 a.m. Can you attend? If so, no reply is needed. If not, please let us know which day suits you. We will try to move the meeting, and if we can do so, we will confirm by Wednesday. We can also send you the slides in advance, if you would like us to. Best regards, Tuan

### c1-06-7
