The key word is the last noun, output…\""} → {"kind":"detail","prompt":"According to Tuan, how should you read a long noun phrase?","promptVi":"Theo anh Tuấn, nên đọc một cụm danh từ dài như thế nào?","options":["From the first word to the last","Find the main noun, then read each noun stack backwards from its last noun","Only the adjectives","By translating every word"],"answerIndex":1,"explanationVi":"Anh nói \"Find the main noun first: rebound…\", rồi \"read the rest backwards from its last noun, output…\"."}

### c2-04-4
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Cụm danh từ | = mệnh đề
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Câu → danh từ hoá | Rich countries tightened policy sharply. | → the sharp tightening of policy in rich countries
    - Câu → danh từ hoá | Foreign investors hold fewer bonds every week. | → the steady weekly decline in foreign holdings of bonds
    - Danh từ + phân từ V3 | small exporters hit by weaker demand | = small exporters who have been hit by weaker demand
    - Đồng vị ngữ | capital flight, the sudden mass withdrawal of money | (cụm danh từ đứng sau dấu phẩy để giải thích)
  - Sau:
    - Danh từ hoá: V + trạng từ | the sharp tightening of policy in rich countries | Rich countries tightened policy sharply.
    - Danh từ hoá: chuỗi bổ ngữ | the steady weekly decline in foreign holdings of bonds | Foreign investors hold fewer bonds every week.
    - Danh từ + phân từ V3 | small exporters hit by weaker demand | small exporters who have been hit by weaker demand
    - Đồng vị ngữ (sau dấu phẩy) | capital flight, the sudden mass withdrawal of money | investors suddenly pull large amounts of money out
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** [" of "," rise in "," decline in "," hit by ","-term ","-page ","-month "," for small "] → ["rise in","decline in","tightening of","expansion of","review of","hit by","ten-year","six-month"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 3 cụm danh từ phức (danh từ hoá + of/in, tính từ ghép, danh từ + phân từ, đồng vị ngữ) và một câu kết đơn giản" → "Có cụm danh từ hoá (vd. the rise in…, the tightening of…, a ten-year mortgage)"

### c2-04-5
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Không viết
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Danh từ + danh từ (bổ ngữ trước) | the global shipping freight index | không: the global shippings freights index
    - Tính từ ghép có số | a two-week closure | không: a two-weeks closure
    - Danh từ + cụm giới từ / phân từ | a shortage of empty containers; an inventory bought at peak prices | không: an inventory which bought at peak prices
    - Cụm danh từ ↔ mệnh đề | the rapid rise in freight rates | = freight rates rose rapidly
  - Sau:
    - Danh từ + danh từ (bổ ngữ trước) | the global shipping freight index | the global shippings freights index
    - Tính từ ghép có số | a two-week closure | a two-weeks closure
    - Danh từ + cụm giới từ / phân từ | a shortage of empty containers; an inventory bought at peak prices | an inventory which bought at peak prices
    - Mệnh đề → cụm danh từ | Freight rates rose rapidly. → the rapid rise in freight rates | the rapidly rise of freight rates
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** [" cost "," price "," index"," increase"," of "," that "," which ","-week","-month","-year"] → ["cost increase","price increase","rise in","increase in","shortage of","freight index","review of","three-month"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 3 cụm danh từ phức (chuỗi danh từ như the fuel cost increase, cụm có of/in/for, danh từ + phân từ hoặc mệnh đề quan hệ)" → "Có cụm danh từ phức (vd. the fuel cost increase, a rise in…, a shortage of…)"

### c2-04-6
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Không viết
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Chuỗi danh từ (bổ ngữ trước) | consumer price index projections | không: consumers prices index projections
    - Tính từ ghép | a company-wide review; energy-efficient equipment | không: a company-widely review
    - Danh từ + phân từ / to be + V3 | the flour used at the factory; a review to be completed in January | không: the flour which used at the factory
    - Mệnh đề → cụm danh từ | Prices rose sharply. → the sharp rise in prices | không: the sharply rise of prices
  - Sau:
    - Chuỗi danh từ (bổ ngữ trước) | consumer price index projections | consumers prices index projections
    - Tính từ ghép | a company-wide review; energy-efficient equipment | a company-widely review
    - Danh từ + phân từ / to be + V3 | the flour used at the factory; a review to be completed in January | the flour which used at the factory
    - Mệnh đề → cụm danh từ | Prices rose sharply. → the sharp rise in prices | the sharply rise of prices
- **BT3 (fix)** [The report published last week shows higher inflation.] : nhận thêm ["The report which was published last week shows higher inflation.","The report that was published last week shows higher inflation."]; giải thích "Rút gọn mệnh đề bị động thì bỏ cả which và be: the report published last week." → "Rút gọn mệnh đề bị động thì bỏ cả which và be: the report published last week. Hoặc giữ which và thêm was: the report which was published last week."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** [" review of"," cost "," price ","-wide"," of "," for "," used "," that "," which "] → ["company-wide","review of","price index","used by","increase in","rise in","reduction of","cost control"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 3 cụm danh từ phức (chuỗi danh từ, tính từ ghép, danh từ + of/for/in, danh từ + phân từ)" → "Có cụm danh từ phức (vd. a company-wide review of…, suppliers used by…, a rise in…)"

### c2-04-7
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Không viết
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Chuỗi danh từ | the payment processing framework | không: the payments processings framework
    - Tính từ ghép | a five-person team; a Singapore-based fund | không: a five-persons team
    - Danh từ + phân từ | a bowl made by a family workshop; gateways handling card payments | không: a bowl was made by a family workshop
    - Danh từ + mệnh đề quan hệ | the touch its buyers value; sellers who upload documents | không bỏ who khi nó là chủ ngữ: sellers upload documents
  - Sau:
    - Chuỗi danh từ | the payment processing framework | the payments processings framework
    - Tính từ ghép | a five-person team; a Singapore-based fund | a five-persons team
    - Danh từ + phân từ | a bowl made by a family workshop; gateways handling card payments | a bowl was made by a family workshop
    - Danh từ + mệnh đề quan hệ | the touch (that) its buyers value; sellers who upload documents | sellers upload documents
- **BT3 (fix)** [We need a system that can handle two thousand parcels.] : nhận thêm ["We need a system which can handle two thousand parcels."]; giải thích "That là chủ ngữ của mệnh đề (that can handle…) nên không được bỏ." → "Đại từ quan hệ that/which là chủ ngữ của mệnh đề (that can handle…) nên không được bỏ."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** [" made by"," figures"," of "," for ","-border","-based"," that "," which "," who "," using "] → ["cross-border","order figures","made by","placed by","reported by","growth in","growth rate","based in"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 3 cụm danh từ phức (chuỗi danh từ, tính từ ghép, danh từ + phân từ, danh từ + mệnh đề quan hệ hoặc cụm giới từ)" → "Có cụm danh từ phức (vd. cross-border order figures, orders placed by…, growth in…)"

### c2-04-8
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Không viết
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Chuỗi danh từ | a green infrastructure venture capital portfolio | không: a green infrastructures portfolio
    - Tính từ ghép | a coal-fired power plant; a ten-year bond | không: a ten-years bond
    - Danh từ + of/with/for | a coupon of six percent; investments with a measurable impact | không: a six percent of coupon
    - Danh từ + phân từ / mệnh đề | projects backed by government contracts; the reports the bank prepared | không: projects are backed by government contracts
  - Sau:
    - Chuỗi danh từ | a green infrastructure venture capital portfolio | a green infrastructures portfolio
    - Tính từ ghép | a coal-fired power plant; a ten-year bond | a ten-years bond
    - Danh từ + of/with/for | a coupon of six percent; investments with a measurable impact | a six percent of coupon
    - Danh từ + phân từ / mệnh đề | projects backed by government contracts; the reports the bank prepared | projects are backed by government contracts
- **BT3 (fix)** [The bond pays a six-percent annual coupon.] : nhận thêm ["The bond pays a six per cent annual coupon.","The bond pays a six percent annual coupon.","The bond pays a six-per-cent annual coupon."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** [" portfolio"," of "," with "," for "," backed by"," used ","-year","-term"," that "," which "] → ["backed by","signed with","raised from","yield of","long-term","ten-year","fifteen-year","power project"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 3 cụm danh từ phức (chuỗi danh từ, tính từ ghép, danh từ + of/with/for, danh từ + phân từ hoặc mệnh đề)" → "Có cụm danh từ phức (vd. a fifteen-year solar power project, contracts signed with…)"

### c2-05-1
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | = câu thường
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as I admire your honesty, I disagree. | Much as I like him, I can't support him.
    - Tính từ + as + chủ ngữ + V | Minor as the problem may seem, we must act. | Small as it is, it isn't harmless.
    - Admittedly, …, but … | Admittedly, the timing is bad, but we must speak. | Admittedly, it's cheap, but it doesn't work.
    - For all + cụm danh từ | For all our talk about transparency, we hid it. | For all his experience, he didn't notice.
    - Câu 1. Be that as it may, câu 2. | That's fair. Be that as it may, I'm accountable. | It's risky. Be that as it may, we can't wait.
  - Sau:
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as I admire your honesty, I disagree. | Although I admire your honesty, I disagree.
    - Tính từ + as + S + V | Minor as the problem may seem, we must act. | Although the problem may seem minor, we must act.
    - Admittedly, …, but … | Admittedly, the timing is bad, but we must speak. | It's true that the timing is bad, but we must speak.
    - For all + cụm danh từ | For all our talk about transparency, we hid it. | Despite all our talk about transparency, we hid it.
    - Be that as it may, … | That's fair. Be that as it may, I'm accountable. | That's fair. Even so, I'm accountable.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Much as","much as"," as it may","Be that as it may","be that as it may","Admittedly","For all","Try as"] → ["much as","admittedly","be that as it may","as it may seem","as it may sound","for all its","for all their","try as i might"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Much as… / tính từ + as… / Admittedly… but / For all… / Be that as it may)" → "Có ít nhất 1 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Much as… / Admittedly…, but / Be that as it may / Strange as it may seem… / For all its… / Try as I might)"

### c2-05-2
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | = câu thường
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Tính từ + as + chủ ngữ + be | Useful as the system is, it should only advise. | Impressive as they are, numbers can't settle it.
    - Admittedly, …, but … | Admittedly, it is fast, but it learned old prejudice. | Admittedly, it helps, but it isn't fair.
    - Động từ + as + chủ ngữ + might | Try as we might, the bias remained. | Try as we might, we couldn't remove it.
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as I trust the data, I want to check it. | Much as I'd like to, I can't blame the doctor.
    - Câu 1. Be that as it may, câu 2. | They signed a form. Be that as it may, nobody read it. | It's legal. Be that as it may, it isn't right.
  - Sau:
    - Tính từ + as + S + be | Useful as the system is, it should only advise. | Although the system is useful, it should only advise.
    - Admittedly, …, but … | Admittedly, it is fast, but it learned old prejudice. | It's true that it is fast, but it learned old prejudice.
    - Động từ + as + S + might | Try as we might, the bias remained. | However hard we tried, the bias remained.
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as I trust the data, I want to check it. | Although I trust the data, I want to check it.
    - Be that as it may, … | They signed a form. Be that as it may, nobody read it. | They signed a form. Even so, nobody read it.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Much as","much as"," as it may"," as the ","Be that as it may","be that as it may","Admittedly","Try as","For all"] → ["much as","admittedly","be that as it may","as it may seem","as it may sound","for all its","for all their","try as i might"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (tính từ + as… / Much as… / Admittedly… but / Try as… might / Be that as it may)" → "Có ít nhất 1 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Much as… / Admittedly…, but / Be that as it may / Strange as it may seem… / For all its… / Try as I might)"

### c2-05-3
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | = câu thường
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Tính từ + as + chủ ngữ + be | Ambitious as the plan is, it is what we need. | Hard as it is to say, the reef won't recover.
    - Admittedly, …, but … | Admittedly, it brings money, but money can't rebuild a reef. | Admittedly, our road is bad, but we don't need a resort.
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as I want jobs, I want the reef too. | Much as I want jobs, I don't want a dead sea.
    - For all + cụm danh từ | For all its green promises, the plan harms the coral. | For all their money, they can't buy the sea.
    - Câu 1. Be that as it may, câu 2. | It's a trade-off. Be that as it may, some things are a matter of principle. | Jobs matter. Be that as it may, the reef can't be replaced.
  - Sau:
    - Tính từ + as + S + be | Ambitious as the plan is, it is what we need. | Although the plan is ambitious, it is what we need.
    - Admittedly, …, but … | Admittedly, it brings money, but money can't rebuild a reef. | It's true that it brings money, but money can't rebuild a reef.
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as I want jobs, I want the reef too. | Although I want jobs, I want the reef too.
    - For all + cụm danh từ | For all its green promises, the plan harms the coral. | Despite its green promises, the plan harms the coral.
    - Be that as it may, … | It's a trade-off. Be that as it may, the reef can't be replaced. | It's a trade-off. Even so, the reef can't be replaced.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Much as","much as"," as the "," as it ","Be that as it may","be that as it may","Admittedly","For all","Try as"] → ["much as","admittedly","be that as it may","as it may seem","as it may sound","for all its","for all their","try as i might"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Much as… / tính từ + as… / Admittedly… but / For all… / Be that as it may)" → "Có ít nhất 1 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Much as… / Admittedly…, but / Be that as it may / Strange as it may seem… / For all its… / Try as I might)"

### c2-05-4
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | = câu thường
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Tính từ + as + chủ ngữ + V | Successful as you are, you sound exhausted. | Rich as she is, she isn't happy.
    - Strange as it may sound / seem | Strange as it may sound, I envy you. | Strange as it seems, money doesn't help.
    - Admittedly, …, but … | Admittedly, you look happier, but it's romantic. | Admittedly, it pays well, but I don't enjoy it.
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as I admire Aristotle, he never had a deadline. | Much as I love it, it isn't for everyone.
    - Câu 1. Be that as it may, câu 2. | I earn less. Be that as it may, I sleep well. | It's hard. Be that as it may, I won't give up.
  - Sau:
    - Tính từ + as + S + V | Successful as you are, you sound exhausted. | Although you are successful, you sound exhausted.
    - Strange as it may sound | Strange as it may sound, I envy you. | It may sound strange, but I envy you.
    - Admittedly, …, but … | Admittedly, it pays well, but I don't enjoy it. | It's true that it pays well, but I don't enjoy it.
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as I admire Aristotle, he never had a deadline. | Although I admire Aristotle, he never had a deadline.
    - Be that as it may, … | I earn less. Be that as it may, I sleep well. | I earn less. Even so, I sleep well.
- **Dịch T1** [Strange as it may sound, money doesn't bring contentment.]: nhận thêm ["Strange as it sounds, money doesn't bring contentment.","Money doesn't bring contentment, strange as it may sound.","Money doesn't bring contentment, strange as it sounds."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Much as","much as"," as it may"," as I "," as you "," as she "," as he ","Be that as it may","be that as it may","Admittedly","Try as","For all"] → ["much as","admittedly","be that as it may","as it may seem","as it may sound","for all its","for all their","try as i might"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (tính từ + as… / Much as… / Admittedly… but / Be that as it may / Try as I might)" → "Có ít nhất 1 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Much as… / Admittedly…, but / Be that as it may / Strange as it may seem… / For all its… / Try as I might)"

### c2-05-5
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | = câu thường
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Admittedly …, but … | Admittedly, the numbers are small, but the pattern is clear. | Admittedly, it wasn't deliberate, but it was careless.
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as I respect your work, I disagree. | Much as I respect your work, I can't agree.
    - Be that as it may, … | The trial is not finished. Be that as it may, we must act. | Be that as it may, we can't stay silent.
    - Tính từ + as / though + S + V | Tired as I am, I will speak to the board. | Careless as it was, it was not deliberate.
  - Sau:
    - Admittedly, …, but … | Admittedly, the numbers are small, but the pattern is clear. | It's true that the numbers are small, but the pattern is clear.
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as I respect your work, I disagree. | Although I respect your work, I disagree.
    - Be that as it may, … | The trial is not finished. Be that as it may, we must act. | The trial is not finished. Even so, we must act.
    - Tính từ + as / though + S + V | Tired as I am, I will speak to the board. | Although I am tired, I will speak to the board.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Admittedly","Much as","Be that as it may","as it may"," as I am"," as it was"," as it is"," though it","Granted","For all"] → ["much as","admittedly","be that as it may","as it may seem","as it may sound","for all its","for all their","try as i might"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Admittedly…but / Much as… / Be that as it may / tính từ + as / though + chủ ngữ + động từ)" → "Có ít nhất 1 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Much as… / Admittedly…, but / Be that as it may / Strange as it may seem… / For all its… / Try as I might)"

### c2-05-6
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | = câu thường
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Tính từ + as + S + V | Rich as we have become, we see our families less. | Strange as it may sound, I don't miss my old things.
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as I admire your discipline, I disagree. | Much as I like shopping, I can't afford it.
    - For all + cụm danh từ | For all its shopping centres, the city is lonely. | For all his money, he isn't happy.
    - Try as + S + might | Try as I might, I can't argue with that. | Try as she might, she couldn't stop buying shoes.
  - Sau:
    - Tính từ + as + S + V | Rich as we have become, we see our families less. | Although we have become rich, we see our families less.
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as I admire your discipline, I disagree. | Although I admire your discipline, I disagree.
    - For all + cụm danh từ | For all its shopping centres, the city is lonely. | Despite all its shopping centres, the city is lonely.
    - Try as + S + might | Try as I might, I can't argue with that. | However hard I try, I can't argue with that.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Much as","Admittedly","For all","Be that as it may","as it may","Try as"," as we are"," as I am"," as it is"," as it was"," as they are","Granted"] → ["much as","admittedly","be that as it may","as it may seem","as it may sound","for all its","for all their","try as i might"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Much as… / Admittedly…but / For all… / Be that as it may / tính từ + as + chủ ngữ + động từ / Try as I might)" → "Có ít nhất 1 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Much as… / Admittedly…, but / Be that as it may / Strange as it may seem… / For all its… / Try as I might)"

### c2-05-7
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | = câu thường
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Admittedly …, but … | Admittedly, the wages are low, but they are the best here. | Admittedly, it isn't cheap, but it is fair.
    - Tính từ + as / though + S + V | Expensive though it will be, I'll pay more. | Uncomfortable as it is, it's true.
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as it will hurt my profits, I'll do it. | Much as I like you, I can't accept this price.
    - For all + cụm danh từ | For all their skill, they earn very little. | For all our advertising, nobody knows the makers.
  - Sau:
    - Admittedly, …, but … | Admittedly, the wages are low, but they are the best here. | It's true that the wages are low, but they are the best here.
    - Tính từ + as / though + S + V | Expensive though it will be, I'll pay more. | Although it will be expensive, I'll pay more.
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as it will hurt my profits, I'll do it. | Although it will hurt my profits, I'll do it.
    - For all + cụm danh từ | For all their skill, they earn very little. | Despite their skill, they earn very little.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Admittedly","Much as","For all","Be that as it may","as it may"," though it"," as it is"," as it was"," as it would"," though they","Granted"] → ["much as","admittedly","be that as it may","as it may seem","as it may sound","for all its","for all their","try as i might"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Admittedly…but / Much as… / For all… / Be that as it may / tính từ + as / though + chủ ngữ + động từ)" → "Có ít nhất 1 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Much as… / Admittedly…, but / Be that as it may / Strange as it may seem… / For all its… / Try as I might)"

### c2-05-8
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | = câu thường
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as he loves you, he feels hurt. | Much as I love them, I can't stay.
    - Admittedly …, but … | Admittedly, it kept families together, but… | Admittedly, it isn't easy, but it's my life.
    - Tính từ / trạng từ + as / though + S + V | Far though Singapore is, it's only two hours away. | Painful as it is, I won't give up.
    - Be that as it may, … | Be that as it may, your parents are getting older. | Be that as it may, I can't stay here.
  - Sau:
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as he loves you, he feels hurt. | Although he loves you, he feels hurt.
    - Admittedly, …, but … | Admittedly, the old way kept families together, but times change. | It's true that the old way kept families together, but times change.
    - Tính từ / trạng từ + as / though + S + V | Far though Singapore is, it's only two hours away. | Although Singapore is far, it's only two hours away.
    - Be that as it may, … | I want to stay. Be that as it may, your parents are getting older. | I want to stay. Even so, your parents are getting older.
- **grammarStep.questionVi:** "Bạn muốn nói: "Bướng bỉnh như con, con nói giống hệt ông nội." Câu nào đúng?" → "Bạn muốn nói: "Dù khó khăn đến mấy, con vẫn sẽ đi." Câu nào đúng?"
- **grammarStep.explanationVi:** "Tính từ đứng đầu, rồi as + chủ ngữ + động từ theo trật tự bình thường: Stubborn as you are. Không đặt as trước tính từ và không đảo are lên trước you." → "Tính từ đứng đầu, rồi as + chủ ngữ + động từ theo trật tự bình thường: Hard as it is (dù khó đến mấy). Không đặt as trước tính từ và không đảo is lên trước it."
- **Câu hỏi phát hiện quy tắc, phương án:** ["Stubborn as you are, you sound just like your grandfather.","As stubborn you are, you sound just like your grandfather.","Stubborn as are you, you sound just like your grandfather."] → ["Hard as it is, I will still go.","As hard it is, I will still go.","Hard as is it, I will still go."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Much as","Admittedly","Be that as it may","as it may"," as it is"," as it was"," as I am"," as they are"," though it","For all","Granted"] → ["much as","admittedly","be that as it may","as it may seem","as it may sound","for all its","for all their","try as i might"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Much as… / Admittedly…but / Be that as it may / tính từ + as / though + chủ ngữ + động từ)" → "Có ít nhất 1 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Much as… / Admittedly…, but / Be that as it may / Strange as it may seem… / For all its… / Try as I might)"

### c2-06-1
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Nghĩa
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - may / might well + V (rất có thể) | They may well be right. | They may well not agree.
    - might as well + V (thôi thì cứ…) | We might as well write it ourselves. | We might as well not bother.
    - could hardly + V (hầu như không thể) | We could hardly test every cell. | (hardly đã mang nghĩa phủ định, không thêm not)
    - be bound to + V (chắc chắn sẽ) | Mistakes are bound to happen. | It is bound to fail. (thường không dùng với not)
  - Sau:
    - may / might well + V | They may well be right. | rất có thể
    - might as well + V | We might as well write it ourselves. | thôi thì cứ…, đành vậy
    - could hardly + V | We could hardly test every cell. | hầu như không thể (không thêm not)
    - be bound to + V | Mistakes are bound to happen. | chắc chắn sẽ
- **BT3 (fix)** [The family could hardly be expected to wait another twenty years.] : câu "The family could not hardly wait another twenty years." → "The family could not hardly be expected to wait another twenty years."; đáp án "The family could hardly wait another twenty years." → "The family could hardly be expected to wait another twenty years."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["may well","might well","might as well","could hardly","bound to"] → ["may well","might well","might as well","may as well","could hardly","can hardly","bound to"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 modal ghép (may well / might well, might as well, could hardly, be bound to)" → "Có ít nhất 1 modal ghép (may well / might well, might as well, could hardly / can hardly, be bound to)"
- **Đề viết:** "Write a short paragraph about a new medical technology and its risks. Use at least three of these: may well / might well, might as well, could hardly, be bound to." → "Write a short paragraph about a new medical technology and its risks. Use at least two of these: may well / might well, might as well, could hardly, be bound to."

Ngân nói ít ra các nhà khoa học trong nước sẽ không liều với mạng người."} → {"kind":"vocab","prompt":"Trong câu này, \"gambled with\" nghĩa là gì?","promptVi":"","quote":"\"Then its scientists may well get there first, but at least ours won't have gambled with a human life.\"","options":["đem ra đánh cược, liều lĩnh với","chăm sóc cẩn thận","nghiên cứu kỹ","cứu sống"],"answerIndex":0,"explanationVi":"Gamble with something = đem thứ gì đó ra đánh cược, liều lĩnh với nó. ### c2-06-2
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | = câu thường
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Tính từ + as + chủ ngữ + động từ | Strange as it may seem, I trust it. | Hard as I tried, I couldn't find it.
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as I like it, I check every result. | Much as I admire it, I wouldn't let it decide.
    - Admittedly, …, but … | Admittedly, it is fast, but it makes mistakes. | Admittedly, it isn't cheap, but it saves lives.
    - Câu 1. Be that as it may, câu 2. | It might help. Be that as it may, a doctor must decide. | It is useful. Be that as it may, it can't replace us.
  - Sau:
    - Tính từ + as + S + V | Strange as it may seem, I trust it. | It may seem strange, but I trust it.
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as I like it, I check every result. | Although I like it, I check every result.
    - Admittedly, …, but … | Admittedly, it is fast, but it makes mistakes. | It's true that it is fast, but it makes mistakes.
    - Be that as it may, … | It might help. Be that as it may, a doctor must decide. | It might help. Even so, a doctor must decide.
- **Dịch T1** [Strange as it may seem, the algorithm found a small anomaly.]: nhận thêm ["Strange as it seems, the algorithm found a small anomaly.","The algorithm found a small anomaly, strange as it seems."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Much as"," as it may"," as I ","dmittedly","Be that as it may"] → ["much as","admittedly","be that as it may","as it may seem","as it may sound","for all its","for all their","try as i might"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Strange as it may seem / Much as… / Admittedly… but / Be that as it may)" → "Có ít nhất 1 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Much as… / Admittedly…, but / Be that as it may / Strange as it may seem… / For all its… / Try as I might)"
- **Bài viết mẫu:**
  - Trước: Strange as it may seem, I would let a computer look at my scans first. Admittedly, these systems make mistakes, but so do tired doctors at the end of a long night. Much as I trust technology, though, I would never accept a diagnosis without talking to a real doctor. A machine can find an anomaly. Be that as it may, only a person can explain what it means for my life.
  - Sau: Strange as it may seem, I would let a computer look at my scans first. Admittedly, these systems make mistakes, but so do tired doctors at the end of a long night. Much as I trust technology, I would never accept a diagnosis without talking to a real doctor. A machine can find an anomaly. Be that as it may, only a person can explain what it means for my life.
- **Đề viết:** "Write a short paragraph about whether you would trust an AI system with your health. Use at least three concession structures (Strange as it may seem…, Much as…, admittedly… but, be that as it may)." → "Write a short paragraph about whether you would trust an AI system with your health. Use at least two concession structures (Strange as it may seem…, Much as…, admittedly… but, be that as it may)."

### c2-06-3
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Nghĩa
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - may / might well + V | Nations may well deploy them. | They may well not agree.
    - could hardly + V | They could hardly defend themselves. | (hardly đã mang nghĩa phủ định, không thêm not)
    - be bound to + V | Accountability is bound to disappear. | Someone is bound to object.
    - might as well + V | We might as well write the rule now. | We might as well not wait.
  - Sau:
    - may / might well + V | Nations may well deploy them. | rất có thể
    - could hardly + V | They could hardly defend themselves. | hầu như không thể (không thêm not)
    - be bound to + V | Accountability is bound to disappear. | chắc chắn sẽ
    - might as well + V | We might as well write the rule now. | thôi thì cứ…, đành vậy
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["may well","might well","could hardly","bound to","might as well"] → ["may well","might well","might as well","may as well","could hardly","can hardly","bound to"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 modal ghép (may well, could hardly, be bound to, might as well) và 1 cấu trúc nhượng bộ" → "Có ít nhất 1 modal ghép (may well / might well, might as well, could hardly / can hardly, be bound to)"

### c2-06-4
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Nghĩa
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - could / can hardly + V | I could hardly believe my eyes. | (hardly đã mang nghĩa phủ định, không thêm not)
    - be bound to + V | Companies are bound to want it. | It is bound to cause problems.
    - may / might well + V | You may well be right. | It may well not work.
    - might as well + V | Governments might as well regulate it. | We might as well not wait.
  - Sau:
    - could / can hardly + V | I could hardly believe my eyes. | hầu như không thể (không thêm not)
    - be bound to + V | Companies are bound to want it. | chắc chắn sẽ
    - may / might well + V | You may well be right. | rất có thể
    - might as well + V | Governments might as well regulate it. | thôi thì cứ…, đành vậy
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["may well","might well","might as well","could hardly","can hardly","bound to"] → ["may well","might well","might as well","may as well","could hardly","can hardly","bound to"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 modal ghép (may well / might well, might as well, could hardly, be bound to)" → "Có ít nhất 1 modal ghép (may well / might well, might as well, could hardly / can hardly, be bound to)"
- **Đề viết:** "Write a short paragraph about whether healthy people should be allowed to use brain implants to improve their memory. Use at least three of these: may well / might well, might as well, could hardly, be bound to." → "Write a short paragraph about whether healthy people should be allowed to use brain implants to improve their memory. Use at least two of these: may well / might well, might as well, could hardly, be bound to."

### c2-06-5
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | = câu thường
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Tính từ + as + S + V | Distant as the threat may seem, it is real. | Costly as it is, we can't wait.
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as I respect the research, I disagree. | Much as I like him, I won't vote for him.
    - Be that as it may | It is expensive. Be that as it may, we must act. | It is early. Be that as it may, we can't ignore it.
    - Admittedly …, but … | Admittedly, the machines are small, but they improve. | Admittedly, it is cheap, but it isn't safe.
  - Sau:
    - Tính từ + as + S + V | Distant as the threat may seem, it is real. | Although the threat may seem distant, it is real.
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as I respect the research, I disagree. | Although I respect the research, I disagree.
    - Be that as it may, … | It is expensive. Be that as it may, we must act. | It is expensive. Even so, we must act.
    - Admittedly, …, but … | Admittedly, the machines are small, but they improve. | It's true that the machines are small, but they improve.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** [" as it may seem"," as it seems","Be that as it may","Admittedly","Much as "," as they may seem"," as it is,"," as they are,"] → ["much as","admittedly","be that as it may","as it may seem","as it may sound","for all its","for all their","try as i might"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Distant as … may seem / Be that as it may / Admittedly …, but / Much as …)" → "Có ít nhất 1 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Much as… / Admittedly…, but / Be that as it may / Strange as it may seem… / For all its… / Try as I might)"

### c2-06-6
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Nghĩa
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - may / might well + V | Humanity may well turn to space. | It may well not happen this century.
    - be bound to + V | Costs are bound to fall. | It isn't bound to fail.
    - could / can hardly + V | We could hardly manage it. | (hardly đã mang nghĩa phủ định, không thêm not)
    - might / may as well + V | We might as well start here. | We might as well not go.
  - Sau:
    - may / might well + V | Humanity may well turn to space. | rất có thể
    - be bound to + V | Costs are bound to fall. | chắc chắn sẽ
    - could / can hardly + V | We could hardly manage it. | hầu như không thể (không thêm not)
    - might / may as well + V | We might as well start here. | thôi thì cứ…, đành vậy
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 modal ghép (may well / might well / might as well / could hardly / be bound to)" → "Có ít nhất 1 modal ghép (may well / might well, might as well, could hardly / can hardly, be bound to)"

### c2-06-7
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | = câu thường
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Tính từ + as + S + V | Risky as it sounds, we must try. | Strict as the rules are, no system is perfect.
    - Strange as it may sound / seem | Strange as it may sound, I trust the bacteria. | Strange as it may seem, she isn't worried.
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as he admires them, he has doubts. | Much as I like it, I can't support it.
    - Admittedly …, but … | Admittedly, the results are good, but the trial is small. | Admittedly, it works, but it isn't cheap.
  - Sau:
    - Tính từ + as + S + V | Risky as it sounds, we must try. | Although it sounds risky, we must try.
    - Strange as it may sound | Strange as it may sound, I trust the bacteria. | It may sound strange, but I trust the bacteria.
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as he admires them, he has doubts. | Although he admires them, he has doubts.
    - Admittedly, …, but … | Admittedly, the results are good, but the trial is small. | It's true that the results are good, but the trial is small.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** [" as it sounds"," as it may sound"," as it may seem","Admittedly","Much as ","Be that as it may"," as they are,"," as it is,"] → ["much as","admittedly","be that as it may","as it may seem","as it may sound","for all its","for all their","try as i might"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Risky as … sounds / Admittedly …, but / Much as … / Be that as it may)" → "Có ít nhất 1 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Much as… / Admittedly…, but / Be that as it may / Strange as it may seem… / For all its… / Try as I might)"

### c2-06-8
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Nghĩa
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - be bound to + V | Clean power is bound to transform industry. | It isn't bound to succeed.
    - may / might well + V | It may well happen. | It may well not happen soon.
    - could / can hardly + V | We could hardly meet the target. | (hardly đã mang nghĩa phủ định, không thêm not)
    - might / may as well + V | We might as well fund several designs. | We might as well not bother.
  - Sau:
    - be bound to + V | Clean power is bound to transform industry. | chắc chắn sẽ
    - may / might well + V | It may well happen. | rất có thể
    - could / can hardly + V | We could hardly meet the target. | hầu như không thể (không thêm not)
    - might / may as well + V | We might as well fund several designs. | thôi thì cứ…, đành vậy
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["bound to","may well","might well","might as well","may as well","could hardly","can hardly"] → ["may well","might well","might as well","may as well","could hardly","can hardly","bound to"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 modal ghép (be bound to / may well / might well / might as well / could hardly)" → "Có ít nhất 1 modal ghép (may well / might well, might as well, could hardly / can hardly, be bound to)"
- **Bài viết mẫu:**
  - Trước: Clean energy is bound to become more important for Vietnamese factories, because buyers abroad want products with lower emissions. Fusion may well become a real source of power one day, but nobody knows when. We could hardly wait for it and do nothing in the meantime. Since solar and wind are already cheap, we might as well build more of them now, while a small part of the budget goes to research. That way we will not miss the future, and we will not lose the present either.
  - Sau: Clean energy is bound to become more important for Vietnamese factories, because buyers abroad want products with lower emissions. Fusion may well become a real source of power one day, but nobody knows when. We can hardly afford to wait for it and do nothing in the meantime. Since solar and wind are already cheap, we might as well build more of them now, while a small part of the budget goes to research. That way we will not miss the future, and we will not lose the present either.

### c2-07-1
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Không nói
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Such + be + danh từ + that | Such is the pull of Tet that families still clean. | Such was the cost that they did not fly home.
    - So + tính từ + be + chủ ngữ + that | So deep is the nostalgia that they recreate an old Tet. | So busy were they that they never rested.
    - Nơi chốn + V + chủ ngữ (danh từ) | In every living room stands an altar. On it sit five fruits. | (chủ ngữ là đại từ thì không đảo: On the altar it stands.)
    - Tân ngữ + chủ ngữ + V (không đảo) | Banh chung they still wrap by hand. | That custom I don't remember.
  - Sau:
    - Such + be + danh từ + that | Such is the pull of Tet that families still clean. | Such the pull of Tet is that families still clean.
    - So + tính từ + be + S + that | So deep is the nostalgia that they recreate an old Tet. | So deep the nostalgia is that they recreate an old Tet.
    - Nơi chốn + V + S (danh từ) | In every living room stands an altar. On it sit five fruits. | On the altar stands it. (đại từ thì không đảo)
    - Tân ngữ + S + V (không đảo) | Banh chung they still wrap by hand. | Banh chung do they still wrap by hand.
- **BT4 (reorder)** [Banh chung they still wrap by hand.] : đề "Sắp xếp các từ thành câu đúng." → "Sắp xếp thành câu đưa tân ngữ (Banh chung) lên đầu để nhấn mạnh."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Such is","Such was","So ","On the ","In the ","Into the "] → ["such is","such was","so deep is","so deep was","stands a","stands an","sit a","sits a"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 cấu trúc đưa thành phần lên đầu câu (Such is… that / So + tính từ + is… that / nơi chốn + V + chủ ngữ / tân ngữ đứng đầu)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu đưa thành phần lên đầu câu (Such is/was… that / So deep is… that / nơi chốn + stands/sits + chủ ngữ)"

### c2-07-2
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | = câu thường
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as I admire the team, I disagree. | Much as I like it, I don't agree.
    - Tính từ + as + S + V | Strange as it may seem, silence is feedback. | Tired as I am, I won't give up.
    - Động từ + as + S + might | Try as you might, you will fail. | Try as she might, she couldn't sleep.
    - Be that as it may / Admittedly…, but | Be that as it may, we must act. | Admittedly, it isn't easy, but it works.
  - Sau:
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as I admire the team, I disagree. | Although I admire the team, I disagree.
    - Tính từ + as + S + V | Strange as it may seem, silence is feedback. | It may seem strange, but silence is feedback.
    - Động từ + as + S + might | Try as you might, you will fail. | However hard you try, you will fail.
    - Be that as it may, … | It's a risk. Be that as it may, we must act. | It's a risk. Even so, we must act.
    - Admittedly, …, but … | Admittedly, it isn't easy, but it works. | It's true that it isn't easy, but it works.
- **Dịch T1** [Much as I value harmony, I sometimes need to disagree.]: vi "Dù rất coi trọng sự hài hoà, đôi khi tôi vẫn cần phản đối." → "Dù rất coi trọng sự hài hoà, đôi khi tôi cần phản đối."; nhận thêm ["Much as I value harmony, I need to disagree sometimes.","I need to disagree sometimes, much as I value harmony.","Sometimes I need to disagree, much as I value harmony."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Much as"," as it may"," as I am"," as I was","Try as","Be that as it may","Admittedly"] → ["much as","as it may","as it sounds","try as","admittedly","though i was","though i am"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Much as… / tính từ + as + S + V / Try as… might / Be that as it may / Admittedly…, but…)" → "Có ít nhất 1 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Much as… / Strange as it may seem… / Try as… might / Be that as it may / Admittedly…, but…)"

### c2-07-3
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Không nói
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Nơi chốn + V + chủ ngữ (danh từ) | On the bank of the river lies Bat Trang. | (đại từ thì không đảo: On the bank it lies.)
    - Gone + be + chủ ngữ | Gone are the old coal-fired kilns. | Gone is the smoke that once darkened the sky.
    - So + tính từ + be + S + that | So fierce is the competition that we must change. | So cheap are the factory mugs that nobody buys ours.
    - Tân ngữ/bổ ngữ + S + V | The crackle pattern my family has made for years. | That style I don't make any more.
  - Sau:
    - Nơi chốn + V + S (danh từ) | On the bank of the river lies Bat Trang. | On the bank of the river lies it. (đại từ thì không đảo)
    - Gone + be + S | Gone are the old coal-fired kilns. | Gone is the old coal-fired kilns.
    - So + tính từ + be + S + that | So fierce is the competition that we must change. | So fierce the competition is that we must change.
    - Tân ngữ / bổ ngữ + S + V | The crackle pattern my family has made for years. | The crackle pattern has my family made for years.
- **BT4 (reorder)** [Gone are the old coal-fired kilns.] : đề "Sắp xếp các từ thành câu đúng." → "Sắp xếp thành câu bắt đầu bằng Gone."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["On the ","In the ","At the ","Gone ","So ","Such is"] → ["gone are","gone is","such is","such was","lies a","lies the","stands a","so fierce is"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 cấu trúc đưa thành phần lên đầu câu (nơi chốn + V + chủ ngữ / Gone are… / So + tính từ + is… that / tân ngữ đứng đầu)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu đưa thành phần lên đầu câu (Gone are… / Such is… that / nơi chốn + lies/stands + chủ ngữ)"

### c2-07-4
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | = câu thường
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as I want him to speak Vietnamese, I won't force him. | Much as I tried, I couldn't.
    - Tính từ + as + S + V | Common as that pattern is, it is not a reason to give up. | Hard as it is, don't give up.
    - Try as + S + might | Try as you might, rules alone won't work. | Try as she might, she couldn't remember.
    - Be that as it may / Admittedly…, but | Be that as it may, laughter is not rejection. | Admittedly, it isn't easy, but it matters.
  - Sau:
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as I want him to speak Vietnamese, I won't force him. | Although I want him to speak Vietnamese, I won't force him.
    - Tính từ + as + S + V | Common as that pattern is, it is not a reason to give up. | Although that pattern is common, it is not a reason to give up.
    - Try as + S + might | Try as you might, rules alone won't work. | However hard you try, rules alone won't work.
    - Be that as it may, … | He answers in English. Be that as it may, laughter is not rejection. | He answers in English. Even so, laughter is not rejection.
    - Admittedly, …, but … | Admittedly, it isn't easy, but it matters. | It's true that it isn't easy, but it matters.
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Much as"," as it may"," as it is"," as they are","Try as","Be that as it may","Admittedly"] → ["much as","as it may","as it sounds","try as","admittedly","common as","strange as"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Much as… / tính từ + as + S + V / Try as… might / Be that as it may / Admittedly…, but…)" → "Có ít nhất 1 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Much as… / Strange as it may sound… / Try as… might / Be that as it may / Admittedly…, but…)"

### c2-07-5
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Không nói
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - So + tính từ + be + S + that | So aromatic is the broth that you smell it first. | So salty was the soup that nobody finished it.
    - Such + be + S + that | Such is the pressure that many give up. | Such was the crowd that we couldn't get in.
    - Cụm nơi chốn + V + S | On every table stands a plate of herbs. | (đại từ thì không đảo: On the table it stands.)
    - Tân ngữ + S + V | That I expected. | The bowl itself I refuse to change.
  - Sau:
    - So + tính từ + be + S + that | So aromatic is the broth that you smell it first. | So aromatic the broth is that you smell it first.
    - Such + be + S + that | Such is the pressure that many give up. | Such the pressure is that many give up.
    - Cụm nơi chốn + V + S | On every table stands a plate of herbs. | On every table stands it. (đại từ thì không đảo)
    - Tân ngữ + S + V | The bowl itself I refuse to change. | The bowl itself do I refuse to change.
- **BT4 (reorder)** [That I expected.] : đề "Sắp xếp các từ thành câu đúng." → "Sắp xếp thành câu đưa tân ngữ (That) lên đầu."
- **Dịch T1** [So good was the broth that day that I finished everything.]: nhận thêm ["So good was the broth that I finished everything that day.","The broth was so good that I finished everything that day."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["So ","Such is","Such was","On every","On the table","Next to","itself I","This I","That I"] → ["so good was","so popular is","such is","such was","stands a","sits a","stood a","sat a"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 cấu trúc đưa thành phần lên đầu câu (So … is … that… / Such is … that… / On the table stands… / tân ngữ đứng đầu)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu đưa thành phần lên đầu câu (So good was… that / Such is… that / nơi chốn + stands/sits + chủ ngữ)"

### c2-07-6
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | = câu thường
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as I envy your freedom, I couldn't live like that. | Much as I love Hanoi, I don't want to go back yet.
    - Tính từ + as / though + S + V | Strange as it may seem, I feel most Vietnamese at 4 a.m. | Nostalgic though I am, I won't return.
    - Admittedly …, but … | Admittedly, I travel a lot, but I rarely make friends. | Admittedly, it isn't easy, but it is worth it.
    - Be that as it may | Life abroad is lonely. Be that as it may, I'm happy. | Be that as it may, I'm not unhappy.
  - Sau:
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as I envy your freedom, I couldn't live like that. | Although I envy your freedom, I couldn't live like that.
    - Tính từ + as / though + S + V | Nostalgic though I am, I won't return. | Although I am nostalgic, I won't return.
    - Admittedly, …, but … | Admittedly, I travel a lot, but I rarely make friends. | It's true that I travel a lot, but I rarely make friends.
    - Be that as it may, … | Life abroad is lonely. Be that as it may, I'm happy. | Life abroad is lonely. Even so, I'm happy.
- **Dịch T2** [Nostalgic though I am, I know the city has changed.]: nhận thêm ["Though I am nostalgic, I know the city has changed.","I know the city has changed, nostalgic though I am.","I know the city has changed, though I am nostalgic."]
- **Dịch T3** [Last year I asked my mother for her recipe.]: vi "Năm ngoái tôi đã xin mẹ công thức nấu ăn của bà." → "Năm ngoái tôi đã xin mẹ công thức nấu ăn của mẹ."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Much as"," as it "," though I ","Admittedly","Be that as it may","Strange as","Hard as","Exciting as"] → ["much as","as it may","as it sounds","admittedly","exciting as","strange as","nostalgic though"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Much as… / tính từ + as / though… / Admittedly…, but… / Be that as it may)" → "Có ít nhất 1 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Much as… / Exciting as it sounds… / Admittedly…, but… / Be that as it may)"

### c2-07-7
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | Không nói
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Cụm nơi chốn + V + S | Behind the curtain hang thirty gowns. | Last in the row hangs a white ao dai.
    - So + tính từ + be + S + that | So nervous is Hoa that she can't sleep. | So heavy was the silk that nobody could run.
    - Such + be + S + that | Such is its power that it became an emblem. | Such was the noise that we didn't hear him.
    - Tân ngữ + S + V | This one I made from hemp. | That question I can't answer.
  - Sau:
    - Cụm nơi chốn + V + S | Behind the curtain hang thirty gowns. | Behind the curtain hang they. (đại từ thì không đảo)
    - So + tính từ + be + S + that | So nervous is Hoa that she can't sleep. | So nervous Hoa is that she can't sleep.
    - Such + be + S + that | Such is its power that it became an emblem. | Such its power is that it became an emblem.
    - Tân ngữ + S + V | This one I made from hemp. | This one did I make from hemp.
- **BT2 (choice)** : câu "Such ___ the power of that shape that it became an emblem." → "Such ___ the power of that shape that it has become an emblem."
- **BT4 (reorder)** [This one I made from hemp.] : đề "Sắp xếp các từ thành câu đúng." → "Sắp xếp thành câu đưa tân ngữ (This one) lên đầu."
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["So ","Such is","Such was","On the stage","Behind ","In the centre","Last in","This I","This one I","That I"] → ["such is","such was","so light was","so nervous was","stood a","stands a","sits a","this i believe"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 cấu trúc đưa thành phần lên đầu câu (cụm nơi chốn + V + S / So … is … that… / Such is … that… / tân ngữ đứng đầu)" → "Có ít nhất 1 câu đưa thành phần lên đầu câu (Such is… that / So light was… that / nơi chốn + stood/stands + chủ ngữ / This I believe)"

### c2-07-8
- **Bảng ngữ pháp** — cột: Cấu trúc | Ví dụ | = câu thường
  - Trước (cột "Chủ ngữ | Khẳng định | Phủ định"):
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as I love Hanoi, the first month was hard. | Much as I miss Tokyo, I won't go back.
    - Tính từ + as / though + S + V | Ridiculous as it sounds, I missed Tokyo. | Strange as it may seem, I didn't miss pho.
    - Admittedly …, but … | Admittedly, it is tiring, but it is fair. | Admittedly, it isn't easy, but I'm trying.
    - Be that as it may | They don't understand. Be that as it may, I'm happy. | Be that as it may, I won't give up.
  - Sau:
    - Much as + mệnh đề | Much as I love Hanoi, the first month was hard. | Although I love Hanoi, the first month was hard.
    - Tính từ + as / though + S + V | Ridiculous as it sounds, I missed Tokyo. | It sounds ridiculous, but I missed Tokyo.
    - Admittedly, …, but … | Admittedly, it is tiring, but it is fair. | It's true that it is tiring, but it is fair.
    - Be that as it may, … | They don't understand. Be that as it may, I'm happy. | They don't understand. Even so, I'm happy.
- **BT2 (choice)** : phương án ["Admittedly","Much as","Be that as it may","However much"] → ["Admittedly","Much as","Although","However much"]; giải thích "Admittedly …, but … thừa nhận trước rồi phản bác; các cấu trúc kia không đi với but hoặc cần mệnh đề." → "Admittedly …, but … thừa nhận trước rồi phản bác; Much as / However much phải có mệnh đề theo sau, còn Although là liên từ nên không đứng một mình trước dấu phẩy."
- **Dịch T2** [Much as I love Hanoi, I suffered reverse culture shock at first.]: nhận thêm ["I suffered reverse culture shock at first, much as I love Hanoi.","At first I suffered reverse culture shock, much as I love Hanoi."]
- **Chấm bài viết, cụm dò:** ["Much as"," as it "," though I ","Admittedly","Be that as it may","Strange as","Ridiculous as","Hard as"] → ["much as","as it may","as it sounds","admittedly","strange as","ridiculous as","workaholic though"]
- **Nhãn yêu cầu:** "Có ít nhất 2 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Much as… / tính từ + as / though… / Admittedly…, but… / Be that as it may)" → "Có ít nhất 1 cấu trúc nhượng bộ (Much as… / Strange as it may seem… / Admittedly…, but… / Be that as it may)"
- **Bài viết mẫu:**
  - Trước: Last year I came back to Hue after three years in Melbourne. Much as I had missed my family, the first weeks were difficult. Strange as it may seem, I missed queuing! Here people pushed in front of me at the market, and I did not know what to say. Admittedly, my relatives were very kind, but they asked about my salary every day. Be that as it may, I am slowly finding my place again.
  - Sau: Last year I came back to Hue after three years in Melbourne. Much as I had missed my family, the first weeks were difficult. Strange as it may seem, I missed queuing! So used to it had I become that I felt lost at every bus stop. Here people pushed in front of me at the market, and I did not know what to say. Admittedly, my relatives were very kind, but they asked about my salary every day. Be that as it may, I am slowly finding my place again.
